SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties

- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
     9	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    10	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    11	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    12	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    13	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    15	
    16	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    17	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    18	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    19	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    20	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    21	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    22	            )
    23	        }
    24	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    25	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    26	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    27	        )
    28	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    29	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    30	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    31	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    32	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        }
    34	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    35	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    36	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    37	        }
    38	        setContent {
    39	            CinerificTheme {
    40	                CinerificApp()
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	    }
    44	}

   158	    var signInSessionId by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
   159	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   160	    var introSnapshot by rememberSaveable(stateSaver = CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver) {
   161	        mutableStateOf(CinerificIntroSnapshot())
   162	    }
   163	    var introView by remember { mutableStateOf<CinerificIntroView?>(null) }
   164	    var welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   165	    var signOutLoadingVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   166	    var showCreateAccountContinuation by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   167	
   168	    fun selectProfile(profile: CinerificProfile) {
   169	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() < welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis) return
   170	        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   171	        signedInProfile = profile
   172	        signInSessionId += 1
   173	        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   174	        showHome = true
   175	    }
   176	
   177	    BackHandler(enabled = showCreateAccountContinuation) {
   178	        showCreateAccountContinuation = false
   179	    }
   180	
   181	    BackHandler(
   182	        enabled = !showHome &&
   183	            !showCreateAccountContinuation &&
   184	            introSnapshot.activeFlowName.isNotBlank()
   185	    ) {
   186	        introView?.handleSystemBack()
   187	    }
   188	
   189	    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   190	        AndroidView(
   191	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   192	            factory = { context ->
   193	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   194	                    introView = this
   195	                    onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   196	                        if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   197	                            introSnapshot = snapshot
   198	                        }
   199	                    }
   200	                    restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   201	                    onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   202	                    onCreateAccountContinue = { showCreateAccountContinuation = true }
   203	                }
   204	            },
   205	            update = { view ->
   206	                view.onIntroSnapshotChanged = { snapshot ->
   207	                    if (introSnapshot != snapshot) {
   208	                        introSnapshot = snapshot
   209	                    }
   210	                }
   211	                view.restoreIntroSnapshot(introSnapshot)
   212	                view.onAvatarSelected = ::selectProfile
   213	                view.onCreateAccountContinue = { showCreateAccountContinuation = true }
   214	            }
   215	        )
   216	
   217	        if (showCreateAccountContinuation) {
   218	            CinerificCreateAccountContinuation(
   219	                draft = CinerificCreateAccountDraft(
   220	                    username = introSnapshot.usernameText,
   221	                    email = introSnapshot.emailText,
   222	                    birthday = listOf(
   223	                        introSnapshot.monthText,
   224	                        introSnapshot.dayText,
   225	                        introSnapshot.yearText
   226	                    ).joinToString(" "),
   227	                    membershipTier = introSnapshot.subscriptionTierText,
   228	                    profile = listOf(
   229	                        CinerificProfile.Steve,
   230	                        CinerificProfile.Martin,
   231	                        CinerificProfile.Janny
   232	                    ).getOrElse(introSnapshot.createAvatarIndex) { CinerificProfile.Steve }
   233	                ),
   234	                onBackToDetails = { showCreateAccountContinuation = false },
   235	                onAccountCreated = { profile ->
   236	                    showCreateAccountContinuation = false
   237	                    selectProfile(profile)
   238	                }
   239	            )
   240	        }
   241	
   242	        if (isTablet && !showHome && introSnapshot.activeFlowName.isBlank()) {
   243	            CinerificTabletIntroAccountActions(
   244	                onCreateAccount = { introView?.showCreateAccountFromTabletHost() },
   245	                onSignIn = { introView?.showSignInFromTabletHost() },
   246	                onForgotPassword = { introView?.showForgotPasswordFromTabletHost() }
   247	            )
   248	        }
   249	
   250	        if (showHome) {
   251	            CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   252	                CinerificMainExperience(
   253	                    isTablet = isTablet,
   254	                    signInSessionId = signInSessionId,
   255	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   256	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   257	                    onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   258	                    onSignOutLoadingStarted = { signOutLoadingVisible = true },
   259	                    onSignOut = {
   260	                        signOutLoadingVisible = true
   261	                        welcomeInputBlockedUntilMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() + 500L
   262	                        signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   263	                        introSnapshot = CinerificIntroSnapshot()
   264	                        showHome = false
   265	                        signOutLoadingVisible = false
   266	                    }
   267	                )
   268	            }
   269	        }
   270	
   271	        if (signOutLoadingVisible) {
   272	            SignOutLoadingOverlay(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize())
   273	        }
   274	    }
   275	}
   276	
   277	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   278	    Home,
   279	    Movies,
   280	    Shows,
   281	    Favorites,
   282	    Settings,
   283	    ProgramDetails
   284	}
   285	
   286	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   287	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   288	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   289	) {
   290	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   291	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   292	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   293	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   294	}
   295	
   296	private data class CinerificDeviceIdentity(
   297	    val manufacturer: String,
   298	    val modelName: String,
   299	    val hardwareModel: String,
   300	    val releaseYear: Int?
   301	) {
   302	    val stableId: String
   303	        get() = Base64.encodeToString(
   304	            "$manufacturer|$hardwareModel".lowercase().toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8),
   305	            Base64.URL_SAFE or Base64.NO_WRAP or Base64.NO_PADDING
   306	        )
   307	
   308	    val displayName: String
   309	        get() = buildString {
   310	            append(manufacturer)
   311	            append(' ')
   312	            append(modelName)
   313	            releaseYear?.let { year ->
   314	                append(" (")
   315	                append(year)
   316	                append(')')
   317	            }
   318	        }
   319	}
   320	
   321	private fun currentCinerificDevice(): CinerificDeviceIdentity {
   322	    val rawManufacturer = Build.MANUFACTURER.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown" }
   323	    val manufacturer = rawManufacturer.lowercase().replaceFirstChar { character ->
   324	        character.titlecase()
   325	    }
   326	    val hardwareModel = Build.MODEL.trim().ifBlank { "Unknown model" }
   327	    val normalizedManufacturer = rawManufacturer.lowercase()
   328	    val normalizedModel = hardwareModel.uppercase()
   329	    val knownModel = when {
   330	        normalizedManufacturer == "samsung" && normalizedModel.startsWith("SM-G990") ->
   331	            "Galaxy S21 FE 5G" to 2022
   332	        else -> hardwareModel to null
   333	    }
   334	    return CinerificDeviceIdentity(
   335	        manufacturer = manufacturer,
   336	        modelName = knownModel.first,
   337	        hardwareModel = hardwareModel,
   338	        releaseYear = knownModel.second
   339	    )
   340	}
   341	
   342	private fun accountDevicePrefix(profile: CinerificProfile): String =
   343	    "account:${profile.name}"
   344	
   345	private fun recordAccountDeviceUse(
   346	    preferences: SharedPreferences,
   347	    profile: CinerificProfile,
   348	    device: CinerificDeviceIdentity
   349	) {
   350	    val accountPrefix = accountDevicePrefix(profile)
   351	    val idsKey = accountPrefix + ACCOUNT_DEVICE_IDS_KEY_SUFFIX
   352	    val devicePrefix = "$accountPrefix:device:${device.stableId}"
   353	    val deviceIds = preferences.getStringSet(idsKey, emptySet()).orEmpty().toMutableSet()
   354	    deviceIds += device.stableId
   355	    preferences.edit()
   356	        .putStringSet(idsKey, deviceIds)
   357	        .putString("$devicePrefix:manufacturer", device.manufacturer)
   358	        .putString("$devicePrefix:model", device.modelName)
   359	        .putInt("$devicePrefix:release_year", device.releaseYear ?: 0)
   360	        .putInt(
   361	            "$devicePrefix:use_count",
   362	            preferences.getInt("$devicePrefix:use_count", 0) + 1
   363	        )
   364	        .putLong("$devicePrefix:last_used", System.currentTimeMillis())
   365	        .apply()
   366	}
   367	
   368	private fun mostUsedDeviceName(
   369	    preferences: SharedPreferences,
   370	    profile: CinerificProfile
   371	): String? {
   372	    val accountPrefix = accountDevicePrefix(profile)
   373	    val deviceIds = preferences
   374	        .getStringSet(accountPrefix + ACCOUNT_DEVICE_IDS_KEY_SUFFIX, emptySet())
   375	        .orEmpty()
   376	    val mostUsedDeviceId = deviceIds.maxWithOrNull(
   377	        compareBy<String> { deviceId ->
   378	            preferences.getInt("$accountPrefix:device:$deviceId:use_count", 0)
   379	        }.thenBy { deviceId ->
   380	            preferences.getLong("$accountPrefix:device:$deviceId:last_used", 0L)
   381	        }
   382	    ) ?: return null
   383	    val devicePrefix = "$accountPrefix:device:$mostUsedDeviceId"
   384	    val manufacturer = preferences.getString("$devicePrefix:manufacturer", null) ?: return null
   385	    val modelName = preferences.getString("$devicePrefix:model", null) ?: return null
   386	    val releaseYear = preferences.getInt("$devicePrefix:release_year", 0)
   387	    return buildString {
   388	        append(manufacturer)
   389	        append(' ')
   390	        append(modelName)
   391	        if (releaseYear > 0) {
   392	            append(" (")
   393	            append(releaseYear)
   394	            append(')')
   395	        }
   396	    }
   397	}
   398	
   399	@Composable
   400	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   401	    isTablet: Boolean,
   402	    signInSessionId: Int,
   403	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   404	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   405	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   406	    onSignOutLoadingStarted: () -> Unit,
   407	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   408	) {
   409	    var destination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   410	    var detailOriginDestination by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) {
   411	        mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home)
   412	    }
   413	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   414	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   415	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   416	    val context = LocalContext.current
   417	    val playbackStatsPreferences = remember(context) {
   418	        context.getSharedPreferences(PLAYBACK_STATS_PREFERENCES, 0)
   419	    }
   420	    val accountDevicePreferences = remember(context) {
   421	        context.getSharedPreferences(ACCOUNT_DEVICE_PREFERENCES, 0)
   422	    }
   423	    var mostUsedDeviceName by remember(signedInProfile) {
   424	        mutableStateOf(mostUsedDeviceName(accountDevicePreferences, signedInProfile))
   425	    }
   426	    var programPlayCounts by remember {
   427	        mutableStateOf(
   428	            playbackStatsPreferences.all.mapNotNull { (key, value) ->
   429	                if (!key.startsWith(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX)) return@mapNotNull null
   430	                val count = value as? Int ?: return@mapNotNull null
   431	                key.removePrefix(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX) to count
   432	            }.toMap()
   433	        )
   434	    }
   435	    var programPlaytimeMillis by remember {
   436	        mutableStateOf(
   437	            playbackStatsPreferences.all.mapNotNull { (key, value) ->
   438	                if (!key.startsWith(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX)) return@mapNotNull null
   439	                val durationMillis = value as? Long ?: return@mapNotNull null
   440	                key.removePrefix(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX) to durationMillis
   441	            }.toMap()
   442	        )
   443	    }
   444	    var mostRecentlyPlayedTitle by remember {
   445	        mutableStateOf(
   446	            playbackStatsPreferences.getString(MOST_RECENTLY_PLAYED_TITLE_KEY, null)
   447	        )
   448	    }
   449	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   450	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   451	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf("") }
   452	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   453	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   454	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   455	    var activePlaybackTitle by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
   456	    var activePlaybackLastTickMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(0L) }
   457	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   458	    var primaryDestinationCanScroll by remember(destination) { mutableStateOf<Boolean?>(null) }
   459	    var portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   460	    var portraitBottomNavHeightPx by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(1f) }
   461	    var showSignOutConfirmation by rememberSaveable(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   462	    var signOutInProgress by remember(signInSessionId) { mutableStateOf(false) }
   463	    val destinationStateHolder = rememberSaveableStateHolder()
   464	    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
   465	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   466	    val isPortrait = configuration.screenHeightDp > configuration.screenWidthDp
   467	    val usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav = destination in setOf(
   468	        CinerificDestination.Home,
   469	        CinerificDestination.Movies,
   470	        CinerificDestination.Shows,
   471	        CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   472	        CinerificDestination.Settings
   473	    )
   474	
   475	    LaunchedEffect(signInSessionId, signedInProfile) {
   476	        recordAccountDeviceUse(
   477	            preferences = accountDevicePreferences,
   478	            profile = signedInProfile,
   479	            device = currentCinerificDevice()
   480	        )
   481	        mostUsedDeviceName = mostUsedDeviceName(accountDevicePreferences, signedInProfile)
   482	    }
   483	    val portraitBottomNavScrollConnection = remember(
   484	        isPortrait,
   485	        destination,
   486	        primaryDestinationCanScroll,
   487	        portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   488	    ) {
   489	        object : NestedScrollConnection {
   490	            override fun onPostScroll(
   491	                consumed: Offset,
   492	                available: Offset,
   493	                source: NestedScrollSource
   494	            ): Offset {
   495	                if (
   496	                    isPortrait &&
   497	                    usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   498	                    primaryDestinationCanScroll == true &&
   499	                    consumed.y != 0f
   500	                ) {
   501	                    portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = (
   502	                        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction +
   503	                            consumed.y * PORTRAIT_BOTTOM_NAV_SCROLL_MULTIPLIER /
   504	                            portraitBottomNavHeightPx
   505	                        ).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   506	                }
   507	                return Offset.Zero
   508	            }
   509	        }
   510	    }
   511	
   512	    LaunchedEffect(destination, isPortrait, primaryDestinationCanScroll) {
   513	        portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction = if (
   514	            isPortrait &&
   515	            usesScrollLinkedPortraitBottomNav &&
   516	            primaryDestinationCanScroll != false
   517	        ) {
   518	            1f
   519	        } else {
   520	            0f
   521	        }
   522	    }
   523	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   524	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   525	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   526	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   527	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   528	            },
   529	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   530	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   531	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   532	            }
   533	        )
   534	    }
   535	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   536	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   537	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   538	        catalogRouteModeName
   539	    ) {
   540	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   541	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   542	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   543	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   544	        )
   545	    }
   546	    val mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle = remember(
   547	        programPlayCounts,
   548	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle
   549	    ) {
   550	        val highestPlayCount = programPlayCounts.values.maxOrNull()
   551	        if (highestPlayCount == null) {
   552	            null
   553	        } else {
   554	            mostRecentlyPlayedTitle?.takeIf { title ->
   555	                programPlayCounts[title] == highestPlayCount
   556	            } ?: programPlayCounts.entries.firstOrNull { (_, count) ->
   557	                count == highestPlayCount
   558	            }?.key
   559	        }
   560	    }
   561	    val mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount = mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle?.let { title ->
   562	        programPlayCounts[title]
   563	    }
   564	    val longestPlaytimeProgramTitle = remember(
   565	        programPlaytimeMillis,
   566	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle
   567	    ) {
   568	        val longestDuration = programPlaytimeMillis.values.maxOrNull()
   569	        if (longestDuration == null) {
   570	            null
   571	        } else {
   572	            mostRecentlyPlayedTitle?.takeIf { title ->
   573	                programPlaytimeMillis[title] == longestDuration
   574	            } ?: programPlaytimeMillis.entries.firstOrNull { (_, duration) ->
   575	                duration == longestDuration
   576	            }?.key
   577	        }
   578	    }
   579	    val longestPlaytimeMillis = longestPlaytimeProgramTitle?.let { title ->
   580	        programPlaytimeMillis[title]
   581	    }
   582	
   583	    LaunchedEffect(activePlaybackTitle) {
   584	        val title = activePlaybackTitle ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   585	        while (activePlaybackTitle == title) {
   586	            delay(1_000L)
   587	            if (activePlaybackTitle != title) break
   588	            val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   589	            val elapsedMillis = (now - activePlaybackLastTickMillis).coerceAtLeast(0L)
   590	            activePlaybackLastTickMillis = now
   591	            val updatedDuration = (programPlaytimeMillis[title] ?: 0L) + elapsedMillis
   592	            programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis + (title to updatedDuration)
   593	            playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   594	                .putLong(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedDuration)
   595	                .apply()
   596	        }
   597	    }
   598	
   599	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   600	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   601	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   602	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   603	    }
   604	
   605	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   606	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   607	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   608	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   609	        if (
   610	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   611	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   612	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   613	        ) {
   614	            onSignOut()
   615	        }
   616	    }
   617	
   618	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   619	        if (destination != CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails) {
   620	            detailOriginDestination = destination
   621	        }
   622	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   623	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   624	    }
   625	
   626	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   627	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   628	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   629	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   630	        destination = route.destination
   631	    }
   632	
   633	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   634	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   635	    }
   636	
   637	    fun recordProgramPlay(title: String) {
   638	        val updatedCount = (programPlayCounts[title] ?: 0) + 1
   639	        programPlayCounts = programPlayCounts + (title to updatedCount)
   640	        mostRecentlyPlayedTitle = title
   641	        playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   642	            .putInt(PLAYBACK_COUNT_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedCount)
   643	            .putString(MOST_RECENTLY_PLAYED_TITLE_KEY, title)
   644	            .apply()
   645	    }
   646	
   647	    fun startProgramPlaybackTimer(title: String) {
   648	        activePlaybackTitle = title
   649	        activePlaybackLastTickMillis = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   650	    }
   651	
   652	    fun stopProgramPlaybackTimer(title: String) {
   653	        if (activePlaybackTitle != title) return
   654	        val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
   655	        val elapsedMillis = (now - activePlaybackLastTickMillis).coerceAtLeast(0L)
   656	        val updatedDuration = (programPlaytimeMillis[title] ?: 0L) + elapsedMillis
   657	        programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis + (title to updatedDuration)
   658	        playbackStatsPreferences.edit()
   659	            .putLong(PLAYBACK_TIME_KEY_PREFIX + title, updatedDuration)
   660	            .apply()
   661	        activePlaybackTitle = null
   662	        activePlaybackLastTickMillis = 0L
   663	    }
   664	
   665	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   666	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   667	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   668	            return
   669	        }
   670	
   671	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   672	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   673	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   674	            isShow = isShow
   675	        )
   676	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   677	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   678	            return
   679	        }
   680	
   681	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   682	    }
   683	
   684	    fun requestSignOut() {
   685	        if (signOutInProgress) return
   686	        showSignOutConfirmation = true
   687	    }
   688	
   689	    fun confirmSignOut() {
   690	        if (signOutInProgress) return
   691	        showSignOutConfirmation = false
   692	        signOutInProgress = true
   693	        onSignOutLoadingStarted()
   694	        scope.launch {
   695	            delay(SIGN_OUT_LOADING_MS)
   696	            onSignOut()
   697	        }
   698	    }
   699	
   700	    BackHandler {
   701	        when {
   702	            signOutInProgress -> Unit
   703	            showSignOutConfirmation -> showSignOutConfirmation = false
   704	            destination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> {
   705	                destination = detailOriginDestination
   706	            }
   707	            destination != CinerificDestination.Home -> {
   708	                clearCatalogRoute()
   709	                destination = CinerificDestination.Home
   710	            }
   711	            else -> requestSignOut()
   712	        }
   713	    }
   714	
   715	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   716	        Box(
   717	            modifier = Modifier
   718	                .fillMaxSize()
   719	                .nestedScroll(portraitBottomNavScrollConnection)
   720	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   721	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   722	                        while (true) {
   723	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   724	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   725	                        }
   726	                    }
   727	                }
   728	        ) {
   729	            destinationStateHolder.SaveableStateProvider(destination) {
   730	                when (destination) {
   731	                    CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   732	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   733	                        onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   734	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   735	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   736	                    )
   737	                    CinerificDestination.Movies,
   738	                    CinerificDestination.Shows,
   739	                    CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   740	                    CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   741	                        destination = destination,
   742	                        signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   743	                        selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   744	                        onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   745	                        autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   746	                        onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   747	                            autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   748	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   749	                        },
   750	                        onSignOut = ::requestSignOut,
   751	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   752	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   753	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   754	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   755	                        mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle = mostFrequentlyViewedProgramTitle,
   756	                        mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount = mostFrequentlyViewedPlayCount,
   757	                        longestPlaytimeProgramTitle = longestPlaytimeProgramTitle,
   758	                        longestPlaytimeMillis = longestPlaytimeMillis,
   759	                        programPlaytimeMillis = programPlaytimeMillis,
   760	                        mostUsedDeviceName = mostUsedDeviceName,
   761	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   762	                        catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   763	                        onVerticalScrollabilityChanged = { primaryDestinationCanScroll = it },
   764	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   765	                    )
   766	                    CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   767	                        programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   768	                        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   769	                        onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   770	                        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   771	                        onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   772	                        onProgramPlayed = ::recordProgramPlay,
   773	                        onProgramPlaybackStarted = ::startProgramPlaybackTimer,
   774	                        onProgramPlaybackStopped = ::stopProgramPlaybackTimer,
   775	                        onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   776	                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   777	                    )
   778	                }
   779	            }
   780	
   781	            if (isTablet) {
   782	                CinerificTabletNavigation(
   783	                    currentDestination = destination,
   784	                    portraitHiddenFraction = portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction,
   785	                    onPortraitBarHeightChanged = { heightPx ->
   786	                        portraitBottomNavHeightPx = heightPx.coerceAtLeast(1f)
   787	                    },
   788	                    onDestinationSelected = {
   789	                        clearCatalogRoute()
   790	                        destination = it
   791	                    }
   792	                )
   793	            } else {
   794	                CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   795	                    currentDestination = destination,
   796	                    portraitHiddenFraction = portraitBottomNavHiddenFraction,
   797	                    onPortraitBarHeightChanged = { heightPx ->
   798	                        portraitBottomNavHeightPx = heightPx.coerceAtLeast(1f)
   799	                    },
   800	                    onDestinationSelected = {
   801	                        clearCatalogRoute()
   802	                        destination = it
   803	                    },
   804	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   805	                )
   806	            }
   807	
   808	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   809	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   810	                currentDestination = destination,
   811	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   812	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   813	            )
   814	
   815	            if (showSignOutConfirmation) {
   816	                if (isTablet) {
   817	                    CinerificTabletSignOutDialog(
   818	                        onConfirm = ::confirmSignOut,
   819	                        onDismiss = { showSignOutConfirmation = false }
   820	                    )
   821	                } else {
   822	                    SignOutConfirmationOverlay(
   823	                        onConfirm = ::confirmSignOut,
   824	                        onDismiss = { showSignOutConfirmation = false },
   825	                        modifier = Modifier
   826	                            .fillMaxSize()
   827	                            .zIndex(20f)
   828	                    )
   829	                }
   830	            }
   831	        }
   832	    }
   833	}

   859	) {

   874	    ) {

   880	        )

   882	            modifier = Modifier
   883	                .fillMaxWidth(0.84f)
   884	                .widthIn(max = 420.dp)
   885	                .border(
   886	                    frameStrokeWidth,

   899	                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp)
   900	            ) {

   909	                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
   910	                )
   911	                Row(
   912	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
   913	                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
   914	                ) {
   915	                    SignOutConfirmationButton(
   916	                        label = "Yes",
   917	                        backgroundColor = ColorFrame2Background,
   918	                        contentColor = Color.White,
   919	                        borderColor = Color(0xFFC86BE0),
   920	                        onClick = onConfirm,
   921	                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
   922	                    )

   924	                        label = "No",
   925	                        backgroundColor = Color(0xFF3A343C),

   966	
   967	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   968	    destinationName: String,
   969	    genreName: String,
   970	    modeName: String
   971	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   972	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   973	
   974	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   975	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   976	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   977	
   978	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   979	        destination = destination,
   980	        genre = genre,
   981	        mode = mode
   982	    )
   983	}
   984	
   985	@Composable
   986	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   987	    requestId: Int,
   988	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   989	    suppressed: Boolean,
   990	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   991	) {
   992	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   993	
   994	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   995	        if (suppressed) {
   996	            visible = false
   997	            return@LaunchedEffect
   998	        }
   999	        if (requestId == 0) {
  1000	            visible = false
  1001	            return@LaunchedEffect
  1002	        }
  1003	        visible = true
  1004	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
  1005	        visible = false
  1006	    }
  1007	
  1008	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
  1009	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
  1010	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
  1011	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
  1012	    )
  1013	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
  1014	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
  1015	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
  1016	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
  1017	    )
  1018	
  1019	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
  1020	
  1021	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
  1022	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  1023	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
  1024	            maxHeight > maxWidth
  1025	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
  1026	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
  1027	        } else {
  1028	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
  1029	        }
  1030	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
  1031	            stageScale = stageScale,
  1032	            modifier = Modifier
  1033	                .absoluteOffset(
  1034	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
  1035	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
  1036	                )
  1037	                .graphicsLayer {
  1038	                    this.alpha = alpha
  1039	                    scaleX = promptScale
  1040	                    scaleY = promptScale
  1041	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
  1042	                }
  1043	        )
  1044	    }
  1045	}
  1046	
  1047	@Composable
  1048	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
  1049	    stageScale: Float,
  1050	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
  1051	) {
  1052	    Box(
  1053	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
  1054	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
  1055	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
  1056	        )
  1057	    ) {
  1058	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1059	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
  1060	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
  1061	            val pointer = Path().apply {
  1062	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
  1063	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
  1064	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
  1065	                close()
  1066	            }
  1067	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
  1068	            drawRoundRect(
  1069	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
  1070	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
  1071	                size = Size(
  1072	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
  1073	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
  1074	                ),
  1075	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
  1076	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
  1077	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
  1078	                )
  1079	            )
  1080	            drawRoundRect(
  1081	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
  1082	                topLeft = Offset(
  1083	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
  1084	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
  1085	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
  1086	                ),
  1087	                size = Size(
  1088	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
  1089	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
  1090	                ),
  1091	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
  1092	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
  1093	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
  1094	                ),
  1095	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
  1096	            )
  1097	        }
  1098	        Text(
  1099	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
  1100	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
  1101	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1102	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
  1103	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
  1104	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
  1105	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1106	            modifier = Modifier
  1107	                .absoluteOffset(
  1108	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
  1109	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
  1110	                )
  1111	                .requiredSize(
  1112	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
  1113	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
  1114	                )
  1115	        )
  1116	    }
  1117	}

  1119	@Composable
  1120	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
  1121	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1122	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
  1123	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
  1124	        p,
  1125	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
  1126	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
  1127	    )
  1128	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
  1129	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
  1130	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
  1131	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
  1132	    )
  1133	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
  1134	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1135	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1136	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1137	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1138	
  1139	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1140	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1141	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
  1142	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
  1143	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
  1144	
  1145	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
  1146	
  1147	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
  1148	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1149	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1150	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1151	                end = baseBounds,
  1152	                amount = logoEntryProgress
  1153	            )
  1154	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1155	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1156	                end = baseBounds,
  1157	                amount = logoEntryProgress
  1158	            )
  1159	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1160	
  1161	            drawFigmaImage(
  1162	                image = images.logoSimple,
  1163	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
  1164	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  1165	                stageTop = stageTop,
  1166	                stageScale = stageScale,
  1167	                alpha = logoAlpha
  1168	            )
  1169	            drawFigmaImage(
  1170	                image = images.logoEyes,
  1171	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
  1172	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  1173	                stageTop = stageTop,
  1174	                stageScale = stageScale,
  1175	                alpha = logoAlpha
  1176	            )
  1177	        }
  1178	
  1179	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
  1180	            val y = avatarYOffset
  1181	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1182	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1183	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1184	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
  1185	
  1186	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1187	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1188	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1189	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
  1190	        }
  1191	    }
  1192	}

  1272	                )
  1273	                .requiredSize(figma(300f, scale), figma(spinnerHeight, scale))

  1385	
  1386	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
  1387	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1388	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
  1389	    stageLeft: Float,
  1390	    stageTop: Float,
  1391	    stageScale: Float,
  1392	    alpha: Float = 1f,
  1393	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
  1394	) {
  1395	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
  1396	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
  1397	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
  1398	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
  1399	
  1400	    if (clipCircle) {
  1401	        val clip = Path().apply {
  1402	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
  1403	        }
  1404	        clipPath(clip) {
  1405	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1406	        }
  1407	    } else {
  1408	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
  1409	    }
  1410	}
  1411	
  1412	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
  1413	    image: ImageBitmap,
  1414	    left: Float,
  1415	    top: Float,
  1416	    width: Float,
  1417	    height: Float,
  1418	    alpha: Float
  1419	) {
  1420	    drawImage(
  1421	        image = image,
  1422	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
  1423	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
  1424	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1425	    )
  1426	}

  1428	@Composable
  1429	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
  1430	    scale: Float,
  1431	    entryProgress: Float,
  1432	    finalProgress: Float,
  1433	    alpha: Float
  1434	) {
  1435	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
  1436	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
  1437	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1438	        end = baseBounds,
  1439	        amount = entryProgress
  1440	    )
  1441	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
  1442	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
  1443	        end = baseBounds,
  1444	        amount = entryProgress
  1445	    )
  1446	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
  1447	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1448	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
  1449	
  1450	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1451	        base = baseBounds,
  1452	        target = simpleBounds,
  1453	        scale = scale,
  1454	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
  1455	        alpha = alpha
  1456	    )
  1457	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1458	        base = baseBounds,
  1459	        target = eyesBounds,
  1460	        scale = scale,
  1461	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
  1462	        alpha = alpha
  1463	    )
  1464	}

  1522	@Composable
  1523	private fun SignInPromptText(
  1524	    text: String,
  1525	    x: Float,
  1526	    y: Float,
  1527	    textSize: Float,
  1528	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
  1529	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
  1530	    scale: Float
  1531	) {
  1532	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
  1533	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
  1534	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
  1535	        else -> Alignment.Center
  1536	    }
  1537	    Box(
  1538	        modifier = Modifier
  1539	            .absoluteOffset(
  1540	                x = figma(x, scale),
  1541	                y = figma(y, scale)
  1542	            )
  1543	            .requiredSize(
  1544	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
  1545	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
  1546	            ),
  1547	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
  1548	    ) {
  1549	        Text(
  1550	            text = text,
  1551	            color = Color.White,
  1552	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
  1553	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
  1554	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
  1555	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
  1556	            maxLines = 1,
  1557	            softWrap = false,
  1558	            textAlign = textAlign
  1559	        )
  1560	    }
  1561	}

  1623	@Composable
  1624	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1625	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1626	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1627	    scale: Float,
  1628	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1629	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1630	) {
  1631	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1632	    Image(
  1633	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1634	        contentDescription = null,
  1635	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1636	        modifier = Modifier
  1637	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1638	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1639	            .graphicsLayer {
  1640	                this.alpha = alpha
  1641	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1642	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1643	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1644	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1645	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1646	            }
  1647	    )
  1648	}

  1650	@Composable
  1651	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1652	    x: Float,
  1653	    y: Float,
  1654	    w: Float,
  1655	    h: Float,
  1656	    scale: Float,
  1657	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1658	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1659	) {
  1660	    Image(
  1661	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1662	        contentDescription = null,
  1663	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1664	        modifier = Modifier
  1665	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1666	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1667	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1668	    )
  1669	}
  1670	
  1671	@Composable
  1672	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1673	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1674	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1675	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1676	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1677	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1678	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1679	
  1680	        Box(
  1681	            modifier = Modifier
  1682	                .fillMaxSize()
  1683	                .background(background)
  1684	        ) {
  1685	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1686	                modifier = Modifier
  1687	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1688	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1689	            ) {
  1690	                content(scale)
  1691	            }
  1692	        }
  1693	    }
  1694	}
  1695	
  1696	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1697	
  1698	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1699	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1700	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1701	    } else {
  1702	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1703	    }
  1704	}
  1705	
  1706	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1707	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1708	}
  1709	
  1710	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1711	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1712	}
  1713	
  1714	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1715	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1716	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1717	        colors = listOf(
  1718	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1719	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1720	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1721	        )
  1722	    )
  1723	}
  1724	
  1725	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1726	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1727	)
  1728	
  1729	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1730	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1731	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1732	}
  1733	
  1734	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1735	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1736	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1737	}
  1738	
  1739	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1740	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1741	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1742	}
  1743	
  1744	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1745	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1746	}
  1747	
  1748	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1749	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1750	}
  1751	
  1752	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1753	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1754	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1755	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1756	    return base + bounce
  1757	}
  1758	
  1759	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1760	
  1761	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1762	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1763	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1764	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1765	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1766	)
  1767	
  1768	private val CinerificIntroSnapshotSaver = listSaver<CinerificIntroSnapshot, Any>(
  1769	    save = { snapshot ->
  1770	        listOf(
  1771	            snapshot.activeFlowName,
  1772	            snapshot.usernameText,
  1773	            snapshot.passwordText,
  1774	            snapshot.confirmPasswordText,
  1775	            snapshot.emailText,
  1776	            snapshot.monthText,
  1777	            snapshot.dayText,
  1778	            snapshot.yearText,
  1779	            snapshot.subscriptionTierText,
  1780	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName,
  1781	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName,
  1782	            snapshot.rememberMeChecked,
  1783	            snapshot.createAvatarIndex,
  1784	            snapshot.portraitProfileIndex,
  1785	            snapshot.confirmEmailText
  1786	        )
  1787	    },
  1788	    restore = { values ->
  1789	        CinerificIntroSnapshot(
  1790	            activeFlowName = values.getOrNull(0) as? String ?: "",
  1791	            usernameText = values.getOrNull(1) as? String ?: "",
  1792	            passwordText = values.getOrNull(2) as? String ?: "",
  1793	            confirmPasswordText = values.getOrNull(3) as? String ?: "",
  1794	            emailText = values.getOrNull(4) as? String ?: "",
  1795	            monthText = values.getOrNull(5) as? String ?: "Mon",
  1796	            dayText = values.getOrNull(6) as? String ?: "Day",
  1797	            yearText = values.getOrNull(7) as? String ?: "Year",
  1798	            subscriptionTierText = values.getOrNull(8) as? String ?: "Subscription Tier",
  1799	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = values.getOrNull(9) as? String ?: "Password",
  1800	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = values.getOrNull(10) as? String ?: "Idle",
  1801	            rememberMeChecked = values.getOrNull(11) as? Boolean ?: false,
  1802	            createAvatarIndex = values.getOrNull(12) as? Int ?: 0,
  1803	            portraitProfileIndex = values.getOrNull(13) as? Int ?: 0,
  1804	            confirmEmailText = values.getOrNull(14) as? String ?: ""
  1805	        )
  1806	    }
  1807	)
