   536	    fun handleSystemBack(): Boolean {
   537	        if (activeMockFlow == null || mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress <= 0f) return false
   538	        closeMockSignInScreen()
   539	        return true
   540	    }
   541	
   542	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   543	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   544	
   545	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   546	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   547	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   548	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   549	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   550	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   551	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   552	
   553	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   554	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   555	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   556	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   557	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   558	        pressedMockField = null
   559	        pressedRememberMe = false
   560	        pressedBackChevron = false
   561	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   562	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   563	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   564	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   565	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   566	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   567	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   568	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   569	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   570	        pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
   571	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   572	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   573	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   574	        activeComposingText = ""
   575	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   576	        focusedMockField = null
   577	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   578	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   579	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   580	
   581	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   582	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   583	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   584	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   585	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   586	        } else {
   587	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   588	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   589	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   590	        }
   591	
   592	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   593	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   594	        mockCreateUsernameRevealProgress = if (usernameText.isEmpty()) 0f else 1f
   595	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   596	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   597	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   598	        confirmEmailText = snapshot.confirmEmailText
   599	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   600	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   601	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   602	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   603	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   604	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   605	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   606	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   607	        ) {
   608	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   609	        } else {
   610	            null
   611	        }
   612	
   613	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   614	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   615	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   616	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   617	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   618	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   619	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   620	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   621	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   622	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   623	        }
   624	
   625	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   626	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   627	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   628	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   629	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   630	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   631	    }
   632	
   633	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   634	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   635	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   636	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   637	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   638	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   639	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   640	        pressedMockField = null
   641	        pressedRememberMe = false
   642	        pressedBackChevron = false
   643	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   644	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   645	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   646	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   647	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   648	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   649	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   650	        pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
   651	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   652	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   653	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   654	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   655	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   656	    }
   657	
   658	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   659	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   660	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   661	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   662	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   663	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   664	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   665	    }
   666	
   667	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   668	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   669	
   670	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   671	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   672	        }
   673	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   674	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   675	            progress,
   676	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   677	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   678	        )
   679	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   680	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   681	
   682	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   683	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   684	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   685	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   686	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   687	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   688	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   689	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   690	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   691	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   692	        updateMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
   693	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   694	
   695	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   696	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   697	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   698	        }
   699	
   700	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   701	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   702	        val createWelcomeTransition = isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()
   703	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   704	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   705	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   706	            if (!createWelcomeTransition && stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   707	                drawWelcomeAvatarStack(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   708	            }
   709	        }
   710	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   711	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   712	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   713	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   714	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   715	                    canvas,
   716	                    outgoingFlow,
   717	                    stage.left,
   718	                    stage.top,
   719	                    stage.scale,
   720	                    1f - switchMotion,
   721	                    formFocusMotion,
   722	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   723	                )
   724	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   725	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   726	                        canvas,
   727	                        incomingFlow,
   728	                        stage.left,
   729	                        stage.top,
   730	                        stage.scale,
   731	                        switchMotion,
   732	                        formFocusMotion,
   733	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   734	                    )
   735	                }
   736	            } else {
   737	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   738	            }
   739	        }
   740	        if (
   741	            logoAlpha > 0.01f &&
   742	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
   743	            usesStickyAuthChrome()
   744	        ) {
   745	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   746	        }
   747	        drawAccountPrompt(
   748	            canvas = canvas,
   749	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   750	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   751	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   752	            yOffset = if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) {
   753	                lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   754	            } else {
   755	                0f
   756	            },
   757	            alpha = avatarAlpha
   758	        )
   759	        drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
   760	            canvas,
   761	            stage,
   762	            mockMotion,
   763	            formFocusMotion
   764	        )
   765	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   766	            drawPortraitStickyBottomBannerForeground(
   767	                canvas,
   768	                stage,
   769	                blackToPurple,
   770	                purpleToSettle
   771	            )
   772	            drawBackChevron(
   773	                canvas,
   774	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   775	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   776	                stage.left,
   777	                if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
   778	                    stage.top
   779	                } else {
   780	                    accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
   781	                },
   782	                stage.scale,
   783	                mockMotion
   784	            )
   785	        }
   786	
   787	        if (
   788	            progress < 1f ||
   789	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   790	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   791	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   792	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   793	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   794	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   795	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   796	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   797	            isMockInputDimAnimating() ||
   798	            isMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimating()
   799	        ) {
   800	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   801	        }
   802	    }
   803	
   804	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   805	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   806	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   807	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   808	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   809	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   810	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   811	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   812	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   813	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   814	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   815	                    portraitAuthScrollStartY = event.y
   816	                    portraitAuthScrollStartOffset = portraitAuthScrollY
   817	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   818	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   819	                        return true
   820	                    }
   821	                    mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = isMockAuthScreenAtBottom()
   822	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   823	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   824	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   825	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   826	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   827	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   828	                    if (
   829	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   830	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   831	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   832	                    ) {
   833	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   834	                        return true
   835	                    }
   836	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   837	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   838	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   839	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   840	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   841	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   842	                    } else {
   843	                        null
   844	                    }
   845	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   846	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   847	                    } else {
   848	                        null
   849	                    }
   850	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   851	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   852	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   853	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   854	                    ) {
   855	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   856	                    } else {
   857	                        null
   858	                    }
   859	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   860	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   861	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   862	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   863	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   864	                    pressedCreateAccountContinue = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   865	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   866	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   867	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   868	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   869	                        mockCreateAccountContinueHit(event.x, event.y)
   870	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   871	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   872	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   873	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   874	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   875	                        !pressedCreateAccountContinue &&
   876	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   877	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   878	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   879	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   880	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   881	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   882	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   883	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   884	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   885	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   886	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   887	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   888	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   889	                    ) {
   890	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   891	                    } else {
   892	                        null
   893	                    }
   894	                    pressedMockField = if (
   895	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   896	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   897	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   898	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   899	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   900	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   901	                        !pressedCreateAccountContinue &&
   902	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   903	                    ) {
   904	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   905	                    } else {
   906	                        null
   907	                    }
   908	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   909	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   910	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   911	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   912	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   913	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   914	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   915	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   916	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   917	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   918	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   919	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   920	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   921	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   922	                        !pressedCreateAccountContinue &&
   923	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   924	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   925	                    return true
   926	                }
   927	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   928	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   929	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   930	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   931	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   932	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   933	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   934	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   935	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   936	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   937	                ) {
   938	                    null
   939	                } else {
   940	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   941	                }
   942	                // This custom view owns the entire welcome surface. Returning false for a
   943	                // slightly imprecise DOWN lets AndroidView's parent cancel the rest of the
   944	                // gesture, so a valid UP never reaches the link or avatar hit test.
   945	                true
   946	            }
   947	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   948	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   949	                    if (updatePortraitAuthScroll(event)) {
   950	                        return true
   951	                    }
   952	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   953	                        return true
   954	                    }
   955	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   956	                        return true
   957	                    }
   958	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   959	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   960	                        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   961	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   962	                    }
   963	                    return true
   964	                }
   965	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   966	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   967	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   968	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   969	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   970	                    }
   971	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   972	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   973	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   974	                    }
   975	                    return true
   976	                }
   977	                true
   978	            }
   979	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   980	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   981	                if (portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
   982	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   983	                    pressedMockField = null
   984	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   985	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   986	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   987	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   988	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   989	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   990	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   991	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   992	                    return true
   993	                }
   994	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
   995	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   996	                    pressedMockField = null
   997	                    pressedRememberMe = false
   998	                    pressedBackChevron = false
   999	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1000	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1001	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1002	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1003	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1004	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
  1005	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1006	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1007	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1008	                    pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  1009	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1010	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1011	                    return true
  1012	                }
  1013	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
  1014	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
  1015	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1016	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1017	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1018	                        return true
  1019	                    }
  1020	                    val requestedFlow = when {
  1021	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1022	                            settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
  1023	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1024	                        }
  1025	                        pressedSignInPrompt &&
  1026	                            settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
  1027	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1028	                        }
  1029	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1030	                            settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
  1031	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1032	                        }
  1033	                        else -> null
  1034	                    }
  1035	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1036	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1037	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1038	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
  1039	                        super.performClick()
  1040	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
  1041	                        return true
  1042	                    }
  1043	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
  1044	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
  1045	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
  1046	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
  1047	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
  1048	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
  1049	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
  1050	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
  1051	                    val releasedCreateAccountContinue = mockCreateAccountContinueHit(event.x, event.y)
  1052	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
  1053	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
  1054	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
  1055	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
  1056	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
  1057	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
  1058	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
  1059	                    val wasPressingCreateAccountContinue = pressedCreateAccountContinue
  1060	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
  1061	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
  1062	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1063	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
  1064	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
  1065	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
  1066	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
  1067	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
  1068	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
  1069	                        pressedOption != null &&
  1070	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
  1071	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
  1072	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
  1073	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
  1074	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
  1075	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
  1076	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
  1077	                    val shouldContinueCreateAccount = wasPressingCreateAccountContinue &&
  1078	                        releasedCreateAccountContinue
  1079	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
  1080	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1081	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
  1082	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1083	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1084	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
  1085	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
  1086	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
  1087	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
  1088	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
  1089	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
  1090	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
  1091	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
  1092	                        !wasPressingCreateAccountContinue &&
  1093	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1094	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
  1095	                        pressedOption == null &&
  1096	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
  1097	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
  1098	                    pressedMockField = null
  1099	                    pressedRememberMe = false
  1100	                    pressedBackChevron = false
  1101	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1102	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1103	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1104	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1105	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1106	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
  1107	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1108	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1109	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1110	                    pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  1111	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1112	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1113	                    super.performClick()
  1114	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
  1115	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
  1116	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
  1117	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1118	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1119	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
  1120	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
  1121	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1122	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1123	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
  1124	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
  1125	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1126	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
  1127	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
  1128	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1129	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1130	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1131	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1132	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1133	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
  1134	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
  1135	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
  1136	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1137	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1138	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1139	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1140	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
  1141	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
  1142	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  1143	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1144	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
  1145	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
  1146	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
  1147	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  1148	                    } else if (shouldContinueCreateAccount) {
  1149	                        if (mockCreateAccountCanContinue()) {
  1150	                            showCreateAccountValidationWarning = false
  1151	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1152	                            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1153	                            onCreateAccountContinue?.invoke()
  1154	                        } else {
  1155	                            showCreateAccountValidationWarning = true
  1156	                            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1157	                        }
  1158	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
  1159	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1160	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1161	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1162	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
  1163	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
  1164	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1165	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  1166	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1167	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1168	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1169	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1170	                        }
  1171	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1172	                    } else {
  1173	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1174	                    }
  1175	                    return true
  1176	                }
  1177	                val tapStayedWithinSlop =
  1178	                    abs(event.x - mockTouchDownX) <= touchSlop &&
  1179	                        abs(event.y - mockTouchDownY) <= touchSlop
  1180	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1181	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1182	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1183	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1184	                    pressedSignInPrompt &&
  1185	                    settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1186	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1187	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1188	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1189	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1190	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1191	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1192	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1193	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1194	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1195	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1196	                    swipeDirection != null
  1197	                val shouldNavigate = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1198	                    !shouldSwipeProfile &&
  1199	                    !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1200	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1201	                    pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
  1202	                clickAvatarProfile = releasedAvatarProfile
  1203	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1204	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1205	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1206	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1207	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1208	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1209	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1210	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1211	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1212	                    true
  1213	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1214	                    super.performClick()
  1215	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1216	                    true
  1217	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1218	                    super.performClick()
  1219	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1220	                    true
  1221	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1222	                    super.performClick()
  1223	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1224	                    true
  1225	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1226	                    performClick()
  1227	                    true
  1228	                } else {
  1229	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1230	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1231	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1232	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1233	                    true
  1234	                }
  1235	            }
  1236	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1237	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1238	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1239	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1240	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1241	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1242	                pressedMockField = null
  1243	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1244	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1245	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1246	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1247	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1248	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1249	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1250	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1251	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1252	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1253	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1254	                pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  1255	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1256	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1257	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1258	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  1259	                portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  1260	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1261	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1262	                true
  1263	            }
  1264	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1265	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1266	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1267	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1268	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1269	        }
  1270	    }
  1271	
  1272	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1273	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1274	    }
  1275	
  1276	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1277	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1278	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1279	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1280	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1281	            MockSignInField.Email,
  1282	            MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1283	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1284	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1285	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1286	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1287	        }
  1288	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1289	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1290	        } else {
  1291	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1292	        }
  1293	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1294	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1295	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1296	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1297	    }
  1298	
  1299	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1300	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1301	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1302	        }
  1303	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1304	    }
  1305	
  1306	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1307	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1308	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1309	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1310	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1311	        }
  1312	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1313	    }
  1314	
  1315	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1316	        super.performClick()
  1317	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1318	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1319	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1320	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1321	        return true
  1322	    }

  1324	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1325	        canvas: Canvas,
  1326	        stageLeft: Float,
  1327	        stageTop: Float,
  1328	        stageScale: Float,
  1329	        progress: Float,
  1330	        alpha: Float
  1331	    ) {
  1332	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1333	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1334	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY()
  1335	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1336	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1337	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1338	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1339	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1340	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1341	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1342	        )
  1343	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1344	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1345	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1346	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1347	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1348	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1349	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1350	        )
  1351	        val authLogoScale = landscapeAuthLogoScale()
  1352	        val base = scaleBoundsFromTopCenter(
  1353	            offsetBounds(
  1354	                lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1355	                focusShiftY
  1356	            ),
  1357	            authLogoScale
  1358	        )
  1359	        val final = scaleBoundsFromTopCenter(
  1360	            offsetBounds(
  1361	                lerpBounds(
  1362	                    Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT),
  1363	                    portraitLogo,
  1364	                    portraitAmount
  1365	                ),
  1366	                focusShiftY
  1367	            ),
  1368	            authLogoScale
  1369	        )
  1370	
  1371	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1372	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1373	            return
  1374	        }
  1375	
  1376	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1377	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1378	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1379	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1380	    }
  1381	
  1382	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1383	        canvas: Canvas,
  1384	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1385	        yOffset: Float,
  1386	        alpha: Float
  1387	    ) {
  1388	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1389	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1390	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1391	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1392	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1393	
  1394	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1395	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1396	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1397	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1398	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1399	            )
  1400	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1401	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1402	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1403	            )
  1404	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1405	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1406	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1407	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1408	            )
  1409	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1410	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1411	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1412	            )
  1413	        } else {
  1414	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1415	        }
  1416	    }

  1418	    private fun drawWelcomeAvatarStack(
  1419	        canvas: Canvas,
  1420	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1421	        yOffset: Float,
  1422	        alpha: Float
  1423	    ) {
  1424	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {

  1427	        }

  1431	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1432	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1433	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1434	
  1435	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1436	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1437	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1438	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)

  1442	    private fun drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1443	        canvas: Canvas,
  1444	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1445	        yOffset: Float,
  1446	        alpha: Float,
  1447	        profileIndex: Int,
  1448	        offsetX: Float
  1449	    ) {
  1450	        val profile = FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[profileIndex].profile
  1451	        val avatarBitmap = when (profile) {
  1452	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveAvatar
  1453	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinAvatar
  1454	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyAvatar
  1455	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestAvatar
  1456	        }
  1457	        val nameBitmap = when (profile) {
  1458	            CinerificProfile.Steve -> steveName
  1459	            CinerificProfile.Martin -> martinName
  1460	            CinerificProfile.Janny -> jannyName
  1461	            CinerificProfile.Guest -> guestName
  1462	        }
  1463	        val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  1464	        val nameWidth = (if (profile == CinerificProfile.Guest) 200f else 220f) * PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SCALE
  1465	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1466	            canvas,
  1467	            avatarBitmap,
  1468	            Bounds(
  1469	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - avatarSize) / 2f + offsetX,
  1470	                portraitAvatarTop() + yOffset,
  1471	                avatarSize,
  1472	                avatarSize
  1473	            ),
  1474	            stage.left,
  1475	            stage.top,
  1476	            stage.scale,
  1477	            alpha
  1478	        )
  1479	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1480	            canvas,
  1481	            nameBitmap,
  1482	            Bounds(
  1483	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f + offsetX,
  1484	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1485	                nameWidth,
  1486	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1487	            ),
  1488	            stage.left,
  1489	            stage.top,
  1490	            stage.scale,
  1491	            alpha
  1492	        )
  1493	    }
  1494	
  1495	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1496	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1497	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1498	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1499	            return
  1500	        }
  1501	
  1502	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1503	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1504	            0f,
  1505	            0f,
  1506	            0f,
  1507	            height.toFloat(),
  1508	            intArrayOf(
  1509	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1510	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1511	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1512	            ),
  1513	            null,
  1514	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1515	        )
  1516	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1517	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1518	
  1519	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1520	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1521	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1522	        }
  1523	    }
  1524	
  1525	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1526	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1527	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1528	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1529	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1530	    }
  1531	
  1532	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1533	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1534	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1535	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1536	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1537	    }
  1538	
  1539	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1540	        canvas: Canvas,
  1541	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1542	        bounds: Bounds,
  1543	        stageLeft: Float,
  1544	        stageTop: Float,
  1545	        stageScale: Float,
  1546	        alpha: Float
  1547	    ) {
  1548	        tempRect.set(
  1549	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1550	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1551	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1552	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1553	        )
  1554	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1555	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1556	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1557	    }

  1559	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1560	        canvas: Canvas,
  1561	        stageLeft: Float,
  1562	        stageTop: Float,
  1563	        stageScale: Float,
  1564	        yOffset: Float,
  1565	        alpha: Float
  1566	    ) {
  1567	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome(activeMockFlow) ||
  1568	            usesStickyAuthChrome(outgoingMockFlow)
  1569	        ) {
  1570	            return
  1571	        }
  1572	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1573	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1574	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1575	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1576	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1577	            portraitAmount
  1578	        ) * stageScale
  1579	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1580	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1581	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1582	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1583	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1584	
  1585	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1586	        val standardCenterY = stageTop +
  1587	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
  1588	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1589	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1590	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1591	            standardCenterY,
  1592	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1593	            portraitAmount
  1594	        )
  1595	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1596	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1597	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1598	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1599	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1600	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1601	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1602	        if (
  1603	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1604	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1605	        ) {
  1606	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1607	            canvas.drawText(
  1608	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1609	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1610	                createBaselineY,
  1611	                accountPromptPaint
  1612	            )
  1613	        }
  1614	        if (
  1615	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1616	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1617	        ) {
  1618	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1619	            canvas.drawText(
  1620	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1621	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1622	                signInBaselineY,
  1623	                formTitlePaint
  1624	            )
  1625	        }
  1626	        if (
  1627	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1628	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1629	        ) {
  1630	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1631	            canvas.drawText(
  1632	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1633	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1634	                forgotBaselineY,
  1635	                accountPromptPaint
  1636	            )
  1637	        }
  1638	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1639	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1640	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1641	    }

  1643	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1644	        canvas: Canvas,
  1645	        stageLeft: Float,
  1646	        stageTop: Float,
  1647	        stageScale: Float,
  1648	        progress: Float,
  1649	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1650	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1651	    ) {
  1652	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1653	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1654	        }
  1655	
  1656	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1657	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1658	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1659	            return
  1660	        }
  1661	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1662	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1663	            return
  1664	        }
  1665	
  1666	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1667	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1668	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1669	        val baseStage = StageMetrics(stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1670	        val usernameY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset
  1671	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  1672	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1673	        val contentStage = mockSignInContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1674	        val contentLeft = contentStage.left
  1675	        val contentTop = contentStage.top
  1676	        val contentScale = contentStage.scale
  1677	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1678	
  1679	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1680	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1681	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * contentScale
  1682	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * contentScale
  1683	
  1684	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1685	        val titleFieldY = usernameY + if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) 0f else focusShiftY
  1686	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
  1687	            canvas.save()
  1688	            canvas.clipRect(
  1689	                0f,
  1690	                authTitleClipTopPx(baseStage),
  1691	                width.toFloat(),
  1692	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1693	            )
  1694	        }
  1695	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, titleFieldY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1696	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
  1697	            canvas.restore()
  1698	            canvas.save()
  1699	            canvas.clipRect(
  1700	                0f,
  1701	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(baseStage),
  1702	                width.toFloat(),
  1703	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1704	            )
  1705	        }
  1706	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1707	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1708	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1709	            canvas,
  1710	            fieldX,
  1711	            rememberMeY,
  1712	            contentLeft,
  1713	            contentTop,
  1714	            contentScale,
  1715	            alpha
  1716	        )
  1717	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1718	            canvas,
  1719	            contentLeft,
  1720	            contentTop,
  1721	            contentScale,
  1722	            alpha,
  1723	            yOffset,
  1724	            focusShiftY
  1725	        )
  1726	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
  1727	            canvas.restore()
  1728	        }
  1729	
  1730	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1731	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1732	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1733	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1734	    }

  1736	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountForm(
  1737	        canvas: Canvas,
  1738	        stageLeft: Float,
  1739	        stageTop: Float,
  1740	        stageScale: Float,
  1741	        alpha: Float,
  1742	        formFocusMotion: Float
  1743	    ) {

  1746	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1747	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)

  1749	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)

  1787	                stageLeft = contentLeft,
  1788	                stageTop = contentTop,
  1789	                stageScale = contentScale,

  1791	            )

  1794	            drawMockSignInField(
  1795	                canvas,
  1796	                field,
  1797	                mockActiveFieldX(field),
  1798	                mockActiveFieldY(field, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  1799	                contentLeft,
  1800	                contentTop,
  1801	                contentScale,
  1802	                alpha,
  1803	                mockActiveFieldWidth(field)
  1804	            )
  1805	        }
  1806	        drawMockDropdownControl(

  2069	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  2070	        canvas: Canvas,
  2071	        stageLeft: Float,
  2072	        stageTop: Float,
  2073	        stageScale: Float,
  2074	        alpha: Float,
  2075	        yOffset: Float,
  2076	        focusShiftY: Float
  2077	    ) {
  2078	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2079	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  2080	
  2081	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  2082	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  2083	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  2084	
  2085	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2086	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2087	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2088	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  2089	
  2090	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  2091	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  2092	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  2093	                canvas,
  2094	                activeDropdown,
  2095	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  2096	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2097	                stageLeft,
  2098	                stageTop,
  2099	                stageScale,
  2100	                alpha,
  2101	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  2102	            )
  2103	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  2104	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  2105	            }
  2106	            return
  2107	        }
  2108	
  2109	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  2110	        drawMockSignInField(
  2111	            canvas,
  2112	            activeField,
  2113	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  2114	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2115	            stageLeft,
  2116	            stageTop,
  2117	            stageScale,
  2118	            alpha,
  2119	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  2120	        )
  2121	    }

  2124	        canvas: Canvas,
  2125	        flow: MockAccountFlow,
  2126	        stageLeft: Float,
  2127	        stageTop: Float,
  2128	        stageScale: Float,
  2129	        progress: Float,
  2130	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  2131	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  2132	    ) {
  2133	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  2134	        activeMockFlow = flow
  2135	        try {
  2136	            drawMockSignInForm(
  2137	                canvas,
  2138	                stageLeft,
  2139	                stageTop,
  2140	                stageScale,

  2145	        } finally {
  2146	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  2147	        }
  2148	    }

  2150	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  2151	        canvas: Canvas,
  2152	        fieldTopY: Float,
  2153	        stageLeft: Float,
  2154	        stageTop: Float,
  2155	        stageScale: Float,
  2156	        alpha: Float
  2157	    ) {
  2158	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2159	        val portrait = isPortraitIntroLayout()
  2160	        val portraitTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stageScale
  2161	        val sourceTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  2162	        val targetTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2163	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  2164	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  2165	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop + (if (portrait) {
  2166	            portraitPromptSignInCenterY()
  2167	        } else {
  2168	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()
  2169	        }) * stageScale
  2170	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  2171	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2172	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  2173	        val targetBaselineY = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  2174	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  2175	            fieldTopY,
  2176	            stageTop,
  2177	            stageScale,
  2178	            targetTextSize
  2179	        )
  2180	
  2181	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  2182	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  2183	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2184	        canvas.drawText(
  2185	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  2186	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  2187	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  2188	            formTitlePaint
  2189	        )
  2190	    }

  2284	    private fun mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  2285	        title: String,
  2286	        contentTopY: Float,
  2287	        stageTop: Float,
  2288	        stageScale: Float,
  2289	        targetTextSize: Float
  2290	    ): Float {
  2291	        formTitlePaint.textSize = targetTextSize
  2292	        val titlePath = Path()
  2293	        val titleBounds = RectF()
  2294	        formTitlePaint.getTextPath(title, 0, title.length, 0f, 0f, titlePath)
  2295	        titlePath.computeBounds(titleBounds, true)
  2296	        val contentScaleRatio = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) portraitCreateContentScale() else 1f
  2297	        return stageTop + contentTopY * stageScale -
  2298	            mockFormFieldGap() * 2f * stageScale * contentScaleRatio -
  2299	            MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TO_FIELDS_EXTRA_GAP * stageScale * contentScaleRatio -
  2300	            titleBounds.bottom +
  2301	            MOCK_FORM_TITLE_OPTICAL_BOTTOM_OFFSET * stageScale * contentScaleRatio
  2302	    }
  2303	
  2304	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  2305	        canvas: Canvas,
  2306	        x: Float,
  2307	        y: Float,
  2308	        stageLeft: Float,
  2309	        stageTop: Float,
  2310	        stageScale: Float,
  2311	        alpha: Float,
  2312	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2313	    ) {
  2314	        tempRect.set(
  2315	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2316	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2317	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  2318	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2319	        )
  2320	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  2321	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  2322	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  2323	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  2324	        val fillAlpha = when {
  2325	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  2326	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  2327	            pressed -> 92f
  2328	            enabled -> 68f
  2329	            else -> 18f
  2330	        }
  2331	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  2332	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  2333	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  2334	            pressed -> 238f
  2335	            enabled -> 210f
  2336	            else -> 118f
  2337	        }
  2338	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2339	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2340	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2341	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2342	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2343	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2344	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  2345	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  2346	        }
  2347	
  2348	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2349	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2350	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2351	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2352	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2353	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  2354	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  2355	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  2356	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  2357	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  2358	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  2359	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  2360	            formTitlePaint
  2361	        )
  2362	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  2363	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  2364	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  2365	            enabled -> 255f
  2366	            else -> 138f
  2367	        }).roundToInt()
  2368	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2369	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  2370	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  2371	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  2372	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  2373	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  2374	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  2375	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2376	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  2377	            canvas.drawLine(
  2378	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  2379	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  2380	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2381	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2382	                backChevronPaint
  2383	            )
  2384	            canvas.drawLine(
  2385	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2386	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2387	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  2388	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  2389	                backChevronPaint
  2390	            )
  2391	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  2392	        } else {
  2393	            tempRect.centerX()
  2394	        }
  2395	        canvas.drawText(
  2396	            buttonText,
  2397	            textX,
  2398	            baselineY,
  2399	            formTitlePaint
  2400	        )
  2401	    }

  2489	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  2490	        canvas: Canvas,
  2491	        buttonRect: RectF,
  2492	        radius: Float,
  2493	        alpha: Float
  2494	    ) {
  2495	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  2496	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  2497	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  2498	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  2499	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  2500	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  2501	            sweepLeft,
  2502	            buttonRect.top,
  2503	            sweepRight,
  2504	            buttonRect.top,
  2505	            intArrayOf(
  2506	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  2507	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  2508	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  2509	            ),
  2510	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  2511	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  2512	        )
  2513	        canvas.save()
  2514	        tempPath.reset()
  2515	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  2516	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2517	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  2518	        canvas.restore()
  2519	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2520	    }
