  2623	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2624	        canvas: Canvas,
  2625	        text: String,
  2626	        centerX: Float,
  2627	        topY: Float,
  2628	        maxWidth: Float,
  2629	        stageLeft: Float,
  2630	        stageTop: Float,
  2631	        stageScale: Float,
  2632	        alpha: Float
  2633	    ): Float {
  2634	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2635	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2636	            canvas,
  2637	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2638	            centerX,
  2639	            topY,
  2640	            maxWidth,
  2641	            stageLeft,
  2642	            stageTop,
  2643	            stageScale,
  2644	            alpha,
  2645	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2646	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2647	            forceLineBreaks = true
  2648	        )
  2649	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2650	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2651	            canvas,
  2652	            middleText,
  2653	            centerX,
  2654	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2655	            maxWidth,
  2656	            stageLeft,
  2657	            stageTop,
  2658	            stageScale,
  2659	            alpha,
  2660	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2661	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2662	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2663	        )
  2664	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2665	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2666	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2667	            canvas,
  2668	            bottomText,
  2669	            centerX,
  2670	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2671	            maxWidth,
  2672	            stageLeft,
  2673	            stageTop,
  2674	            stageScale,
  2675	            alpha,
  2676	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2677	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2678	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2679	        )
  2680	    }
  2681	
  2682	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2683	        canvas: Canvas,
  2684	        text: String,
  2685	        centerX: Float,
  2686	        topY: Float,
  2687	        maxWidth: Float,
  2688	        stageLeft: Float,
  2689	        stageTop: Float,
  2690	        stageScale: Float,
  2691	        alpha: Float,
  2692	        textSize: Float,
  2693	        lineHeight: Float,
  2694	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER,
  2695	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  2696	    ): Float {
  2697	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2698	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2699	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2700	
  2701	        val lines = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  2702	            text.split('\n').flatMap { line ->
  2703	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2704	            }
  2705	        } else {
  2706	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2707	        }
  2708	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2709	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2710	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2711	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2712	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2713	        }
  2714	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2715	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2716	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2717	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2718	        }
  2719	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2720	    }

  2722	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(

  2781	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
  2782	        canvas: Canvas,
  2783	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2784	        mockMotion: Float,
  2785	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2786	    ) {
  2787	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return
  2788	
  2789	        if (isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()) {
  2790	            drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2791	                canvas,
  2792	                stage,
  2793	                mockMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f),
  2794	                formFocusMotion
  2795	            )
  2796	            return
  2797	        }
  2798	
  2799	        val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
  2800	        val transitionProgress = when {
  2801	            outgoingFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> {
  2802	                1f - FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2803	            }
  2804	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && outgoingFlow != null -> {
  2805	                FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2806	            }
  2807	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && isMockSignInAnimating() -> mockMotion
  2808	            else -> return
  2809	        }.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2810	
  2811	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - transitionProgress)
  2812	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2813	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2814	
  2815	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2816	            canvas.save()
  2817	            canvas.clipRect(
  2818	                0f,
  2819	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2820	                width.toFloat(),
  2821	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2822	            )
  2823	        }
  2824	        drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2825	            canvas,
  2826	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  2827	            mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2828	            contentStage.left,
  2829	            contentStage.top,
  2830	            contentStage.scale,
  2831	            1f
  2832	        )
  2833	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2834	            canvas.restore()
  2835	        }
  2836	    }
  2837	
  2838	    private fun isMockCreateWelcomeTransition(): Boolean {
  2839	        return isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  2840	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  2841	            outgoingMockFlow == null &&
  2842	            isMockSignInAnimating()
  2843	    }

  2845	    private fun drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(

  2850	    ) {
  2851	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)

  2855	        val sectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2856	        val settledSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(0f, 0f)
  2857	        val settledContentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, 0f, 0f)
  2858	        val settledAvatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, settledSectionY)
  2859	
  2860	        drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(canvas, stage, motion)
  2861	
  2862	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2863	            canvas.save()
  2864	            canvas.clipRect(
  2865	                0f,
  2866	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),

  2868	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2869	            )
  2870	        }
  2871	        drawMockCreateUsername(

  2874	            contentStage.top + mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(sectionY) * contentStage.scale,

  2876	            1f
  2877	        )
  2878	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2879	            canvas,

  2881	            settledAvatarBounds,
  2882	            settledContentStage.left,
  2883	            settledContentStage.top,

  2885	            motion,

  2887	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2888	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2889	                settledContentStage,
  2890	                motion
  2891	            )
  2892	        )
  2893	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2894	            canvas,
  2895	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2896	            settledAvatarBounds,
  2897	            settledContentStage.left,
  2898	            settledContentStage.top,
  2899	            settledContentStage.scale,
  2900	            motion,
  2901	            mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2902	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2903	                settledAvatarBounds,
  2904	                settledContentStage,
  2905	                motion
  2906	            )
  2907	        )
  2908	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2909	            canvas.restore()
  2910	        }
  2911	
  2912	        val destinationBounds = Bounds(
  2913	            (settledContentStage.left + settledAvatarBounds.x * settledContentStage.scale - stage.left) /
  2914	                stage.scale,

  2916	                stage.scale,
  2917	            settledAvatarBounds.w * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale,
  2918	            settledAvatarBounds.h * settledContentStage.scale / stage.scale
  2919	        )
  2920	        val sourceBounds = mockCreateWelcomeAvatarSourceBounds()
  2921	        val movingBounds = lerpBounds(sourceBounds, destinationBounds, motion)
  2922	        val avatarIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
  2923	
  2924	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  2925	            canvas,
  2926	            createAvatarBitmaps[avatarIndex],
  2927	            movingBounds,
  2928	            stage.left,
  2929	            stage.top,
  2930	            stage.scale,
  2931	            1f
  2932	        )
  2933	    }

  2935	    private fun mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2936	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2937	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2938	        contentStage: StageMetrics,
  2939	        motion: Float
  2940	    ): Float {
  2941	        val settledCenterX = contentStage.left +
  2942	            mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * contentStage.scale
  2943	        val halfChevronWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * contentStage.scale / 2f

  2945	            -halfChevronWidth
  2946	        } else {
  2947	            width.toFloat() + halfChevronWidth
  2948	        }
  2949	        return lerpFloat(edgeCenterX, settledCenterX, motion.coerceIn(0f, 1f))
  2950	    }

  3020	                    stage.scale,

  3049	    private fun drawMockCreateUsername(
  3050	        canvas: Canvas,
  3051	        centerX: Float,
  3052	        baselineY: Float,
  3053	        stageScale: Float,
  3054	        alpha: Float
  3055	    ) {
  3056	        val showPlaceholder =
  3057	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  3058	                usernameText.isEmpty() &&
  3059	                focusedMockField != MockSignInField.Username
  3060	        if (usernameText.isEmpty() && !showPlaceholder) return
  3061	
  3062	        val displayText = if (showPlaceholder) {
  3063	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_PLACEHOLDER_TEXT
  3064	        } else {
  3065	            usernameText
  3066	        }
  3067	        val displayAlpha = if (showPlaceholder) {
  3068	            alpha * MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_PLACEHOLDER_ALPHA
  3069	        } else {
  3070	            alpha
  3071	        }
  3072	
  3073	        val landscapeScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3074	            1f
  3075	        } else {
  3076	            MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_FIRST_IMPRESSION_SCALE
  3077	        }
  3078	        val maxTextWidth =
  3079	            (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_MAX_WIDTH_INSET * 2f) *
  3080	                landscapeScale * stageScale
  3081	        val preferredTextSize = mockCreateUsernameTextSize() * stageScale
  3082	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = preferredTextSize
  3083	        val preferredTextWidth = createUsernameFillPaint.measureText(displayText)
  3084	        val fittedTextSize = if (preferredTextWidth > maxTextWidth && preferredTextWidth > 0f) {
  3085	            preferredTextSize * maxTextWidth / preferredTextWidth
  3086	        } else {
  3087	            preferredTextSize
  3088	        }
  3089	        val paintAlpha = (displayAlpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3090	
  3091	        createUsernameStrokePaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  3092	        createUsernameStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  3093	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  3094	        createUsernameStrokePaint.setShadowLayer(
  3095	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_RADIUS * stageScale,
  3096	            0f,
  3097	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_OFFSET_Y * stageScale,
  3098	            Color.argb((paintAlpha * 0.25f).roundToInt(), 0, 0, 0)
  3099	        )
  3100	        canvas.drawText(displayText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameStrokePaint)
  3101	        createUsernameStrokePaint.clearShadowLayer()
  3102	
  3103	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  3104	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  3105	        canvas.drawText(displayText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameFillPaint)
  3106	
  3107	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = 255
  3108	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = 255
  3109	    }

  3139	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  3140	        canvas: Canvas,
  3141	        bitmaps: List<Bitmap>,
  3142	        bounds: Bounds,
  3143	        stageLeft: Float,
  3144	        stageTop: Float,
  3145	        stageScale: Float,
  3146	        alpha: Float,
  3147	        transitionProgressOverride: Float? = null
  3148	    ) {
  3149	        val carouselProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?: mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  3150	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  3151	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(bounds.w)
  3152	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  3153	        val dragProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?:
  3154	            mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  3155	        val isDraggingAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  3156	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3157	            dragDirection != null &&
  3158	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  3159	
  3160	        fun draw(index: Int, offsetX: Float) {
  3161	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  3162	                canvas,
  3163	                bitmaps[index.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  3164	                Bounds(bounds.x + offsetX, bounds.y, bounds.w, bounds.h),
  3165	                stageLeft,
  3166	                stageTop,
  3167	                stageScale,
  3168	                alpha
  3169	            )
  3170	        }
  3171	
  3172	        if (isDraggingAvatar && dragDirection != null) {
  3173	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  3174	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance)
  3175	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection), direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance)
  3176	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  3177	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex, -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance)
  3178	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex, slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance)
  3179	        } else {
  3180	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, 0f)
  3181	        }
  3182	    }

  3184	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  3185	        canvas: Canvas,
  3186	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  3187	        stageLeft: Float,
  3188	        stageTop: Float,
  3189	        stageScale: Float
  3190	    ) {

  3194	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  3195	            avatarBounds.w * 0.5f * stageScale,
  3196	            Path.Direction.CW
  3197	        )
  3198	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  3199	    }

  3401	
  3402	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3403	        canvas: Canvas,
  3404	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  3405	        left: Float,
  3406	        centerY: Float,
  3407	        iconSize: Float,
  3408	        alpha: Int
  3409	    ) {
  3410	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  3411	        val width: Float
  3412	        val height: Float
  3413	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  3414	            width = iconSize
  3415	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  3416	        } else {
  3417	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  3418	            height = iconSize
  3419	        }
  3420	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  3421	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  3422	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  3423	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)

  3567	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3568	        canvas: Canvas,
  3569	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  3570	        top: Float,
  3571	        right: Float,
  3572	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  3573	        stageScale: Float,
  3574	        alpha: Int
  3575	    ) {
  3576	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3577	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3578	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3579	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  3580	
  3581	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  3582	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  3583	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3584	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3585	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3586	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  3587	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  3588	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  3589	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  3590	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  3591	
  3592	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  3593	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3594	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3595	
  3596	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  3597	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3598	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3599	    }

  3601	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  3602	        canvas: Canvas,
  3603	        field: MockSignInField,
  3604	        x: Float,
  3605	        y: Float,
  3606	        stageLeft: Float,
  3607	        stageTop: Float,
  3608	        stageScale: Float,
  3609	        alpha: Float,
  3610	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  3611	    ) {
  3612	        val visualScale = mockCreateLandscapeFieldVisualScale(field)
  3613	        val visualWidth = fieldWidth * visualScale
  3614	        val visualHeight = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT * visualScale
  3615	        val visualX = x - (visualWidth - fieldWidth) / 2f
  3616	        val visualY = y - (visualHeight - MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) / 2f
  3617	        val originalFieldStrokeWidth = formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth
  3618	        val originalCaretStrokeWidth = formCaretPaint.strokeWidth
  3619	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = originalFieldStrokeWidth * visualScale
  3620	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = originalCaretStrokeWidth * visualScale
  3621	        tempRect.set(
  3622	            stageLeft + visualX * stageScale,
  3623	            stageTop + visualY * stageScale,
  3624	            stageLeft + (visualX + visualWidth) * stageScale,
  3625	            stageTop + (visualY + visualHeight) * stageScale
  3626	        )
  3627	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * visualScale * stageScale
  3628	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3629	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  3630	
  3631	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  3632	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  3633	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3634	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  3635	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  3636	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  3637	            labelProgress
  3638	        ) * visualScale * stageScale
  3639	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3640	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3641	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  3642	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  3643	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top +
  3644	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * visualScale * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  3645	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  3646	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * visualScale * stageScale,
  3647	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * visualScale * stageScale,
  3648	            labelProgress
  3649	        )
  3650	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  3651	        canvas.drawText(
  3652	            label,
  3653	            labelX,
  3654	            labelBaselineY,
  3655	            formLabelPaint
  3656	        )
  3657	
  3658	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  3659	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  3660	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  3661	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  3662	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * visualScale * stageScale
  3663	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3664	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3665	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3666	
  3667	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * visualScale * stageScale
  3668	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top +
  3669	                MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * visualScale * stageScale
  3670	            val maxInputWidth = visualWidth * stageScale -
  3671	                MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * visualScale * stageScale * 2f
  3672	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  3673	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  3674	
  3675	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  3676	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) +
  3677	                    2f * visualScale * stageScale
  3678	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  3679	                canvas.drawLine(
  3680	                    caretX,
  3681	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  3682	                    caretX,
  3683	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  3684	                    formCaretPaint
  3685	                )
  3686	            }
  3687	        }
  3688	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = originalFieldStrokeWidth
  3689	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = originalCaretStrokeWidth
  3690	    }

  3751	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  3752	        canvas: Canvas,
  3753	        centerX: Float,
  3754	        topY: Float,
  3755	        stageLeft: Float,
  3756	        stageTop: Float,
  3757	        stageScale: Float,
  3758	        alpha: Float
  3759	    ) {

  3761	        val chevronScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3762	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  3763	        } else {
  3764	            1f
  3765	        }
  3766	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  3767	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  3768	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * chevronScale * stageScale

  3778	            MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * chevronScale * strokeScale * stageScale
  3779	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  3780	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)

  3803	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
  3804	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
  3805	    }
  3806	
  3807	    private data class CreateAvatarLayers(
  3808	        val bubble: Bitmap,
  3809	        val characters: List<Bitmap>,
  3810	        val bottomStrokes: List<Bitmap>
  3811	    )
  3812	
  3813	    private fun createAvatarCarouselLayers(vararg avatars: Bitmap): CreateAvatarLayers {
  3814	        require(avatars.size == MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT)
  3815	        val bitmapWidth = avatars.first().width
  3816	        val bitmapHeight = avatars.first().height
  3817	        require(avatars.all { it.width == bitmapWidth && it.height == bitmapHeight })
  3818	        return CreateAvatarLayers(
  3819	            bubble = createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(avatars.first()),
  3820	            characters = avatars.map(::createAvatarCharacterBitmap),
  3821	            bottomStrokes = avatars.map(::createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap)
  3822	        )
  3823	    }
  3824	
  3825	    private fun createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3826	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3827	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3828	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3829	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3830	        val strokePixels = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3831	        val minimumBodyY = (bitmapHeight * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_START_RATIO).roundToInt()
  3832	
  3833	        for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3834	            var bodyBottomY = -1
  3835	            for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3836	                if (isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[y * bitmapWidth + x])) bodyBottomY = y
  3837	            }
  3838	            if (bodyBottomY < 0) continue
  3839	            val strokeBottomY = minOf(bitmapHeight - 1, bodyBottomY + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_DEPTH_PX)
  3840	            for (y in bodyBottomY + 1..strokeBottomY) {
  3841	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  3842	                val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3843	                if (sourceAlpha <= 16) continue
  3844	                strokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3845	                    sourceAlpha,
  3846	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3847	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3848	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3849	                )
  3850	            }
  3851	        }
  3852	        val cornerFilledStrokePixels = strokePixels.copyOf()
  3853	        for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3854	            val rowStart = y * bitmapWidth
  3855	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3856	                if (strokePixels[rowStart + x] == Color.TRANSPARENT) continue
  3857	                val sideStart = maxOf(0, x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3858	                val sideEnd = minOf(bitmapWidth - 1, x + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3859	                for (sideX in sideStart..sideEnd) {
  3860	                    val index = rowStart + sideX
  3861	                    val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3862	                    if (sourceAlpha <= 16 || isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[index])) continue
  3863	                    cornerFilledStrokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3864	                        sourceAlpha,
  3865	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3866	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3867	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3868	                    )
  3869	                }
  3870	            }
  3871	        }
  3872	        return bitmapFromPixels(cornerFilledStrokePixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3873	    }
  3874	
  3875	    private fun isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3876	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3877	        val channelMax = maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3878	        val channelMin = minOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3879	        return channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8
  3880	    }
  3881	
  3882	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3883	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3884	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3885	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3886	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3887	
  3888	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3889	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3890	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) *
  3891	            (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  3892	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3893	        val shellPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3894	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3895	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3896	            val dx = x - centerX
  3897	            val dy = y - centerY
  3898	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy >= innerRadiusSquared) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3899	        }
  3900	        return bitmapFromPixels(shellPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3901	    }
  3902	
  3903	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3904	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3905	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3906	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3907	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3908	
  3909	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3910	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3911	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CLIP_RADIUS_RATIO
  3912	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3913	        val distance = IntArray(pixels.size) { -1 }
  3914	        val queue = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3915	        var queueStart = 0
  3916	        var queueEnd = 0
  3917	
  3918	        pixels.indices.forEach { index ->
  3919	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3920	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3921	            val dx = x - centerX
  3922	            val dy = y - centerY
  3923	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared && isAvatarCharacterColor(pixels[index])) {
  3924	                distance[index] = 0
  3925	                queue[queueEnd++] = index
  3926	            }
  3927	        }
  3928	
  3929	        while (queueStart < queueEnd) {
  3930	            val index = queue[queueStart++]
  3931	            val currentDistance = distance[index]
  3932	            if (currentDistance >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_OUTLINE_RADIUS_PX) continue
  3933	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3934	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3935	            for (dy in -1..1) {
  3936	                val nextY = y + dy
  3937	                if (nextY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  3938	                for (dx in -1..1) {
  3939	                    if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue
  3940	                    val nextX = x + dx
  3941	                    if (nextX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  3942	                    val nextIndex = nextY * bitmapWidth + nextX
  3943	                    if (distance[nextIndex] >= 0 || !isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixels[nextIndex])) continue
  3944	                    val centerDx = nextX - centerX
  3945	                    val centerDy = nextY - centerY
  3946	                    if (centerDx * centerDx + centerDy * centerDy >= innerRadiusSquared) continue
  3947	                    distance[nextIndex] = currentDistance + 1
  3948	                    queue[queueEnd++] = nextIndex
  3949	                }
  3950	            }
  3951	        }
  3952	
  3953	        val characterPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3954	            if (distance[index] >= 0) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3955	        }
  3956	        return bitmapFromPixels(characterPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3957	    }
  3958	
  3959	    private fun isAvatarCharacterColor(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3960	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3961	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  3962	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  3963	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  3964	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  3965	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  3966	        val isNeutralBubbleOrRing = channelMax - channelMin <= 4
  3967	        return (Color.alpha(pixel) >= 250 && !isNeutralBubbleOrRing) ||
  3968	            (channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8)
  3969	    }
  3970	
  3971	    private fun isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3972	        return Color.alpha(pixel) > 24 && maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel)) <= 80
  3973	    }
  3974	
  3975	    private fun bitmapFromPixels(pixels: IntArray, width: Int, height: Int): Bitmap {
  3976	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(width, height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  3977	            setPixels(pixels, 0, width, 0, 0, width, height)
  3978	        }
  3979	    }
  3980	
  3981	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  3982	        return when {
  3983	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  3984	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  3985	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  3986	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  3987	            else -> null
  3988	        }
  3989	    }
  3990	
  3991	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  3992	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  3993	    }

  3995	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
  3996	        return IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis > 0L &&
  3997	            bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis) >= 1f
  3998	    }

  4000	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  4001	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  4002	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  4003	
  4004	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  4005	
  4006	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4007	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  4008	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4009	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  4010	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  4011	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  4012	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  4013	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  4014	                ) * stage.scale
  4015	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  4016	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  4017	            } else {
  4018	                null
  4019	            }
  4020	        }
  4021	
  4022	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  4023	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  4024	            val bounds = target.bounds
  4025	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  4026	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  4027	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  4028	            val dx = x - centerX
  4029	            val dy = y - centerY
  4030	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  4031	        }?.profile
  4032	    }

  4290	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4291	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  4292	
  4293	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  4294	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  4295	        if (
  4296	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  4297	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  4298	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  4299	        ) {
  4300	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  4301	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  4302	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  4303	            } else {
  4304	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  4305	            }
  4306	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4307	        }
  4308	
  4309	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  4310	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4311	        }
  4312	        return true
  4313	    }

  4315	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  4316	        if (
  4317	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4318	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  4319	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  4320	        ) {
  4321	            return null
  4322	        }
  4323	
  4324	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  4325	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  4326	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4327	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4328	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4329	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  4330	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4331	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  4332	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4333	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  4334	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  4335	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  4336	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  4337	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  4338	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  4339	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  4340	        }
  4341	
  4342	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  4343	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  4344	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  4345	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  4346	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  4347	        }
  4348	        return null
  4349	    }
  4350	
  4351	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {
  4352	        if (
  4353	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4354	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  4355	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  4356	        ) {
  4357	            return null
  4358	        }
  4359	
  4360	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  4361	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  4362	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4363	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4364	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4365	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  4366	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4367	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  4368	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4369	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  4370	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  4371	        val top = stage.top + yStart * stage.scale
  4372	        val bottom = top + mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) * mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale
  4373	        return if (pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom) dropdown else null
  4374	    }
  4375	
  4376	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4377	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  4378	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  4379	
  4380	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  4381	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4382	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4383	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4384	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4385	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  4386	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  4387	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  4388	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  4389	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4390	        }
  4391	
  4392	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  4393	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  4394	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  4395	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4396	        }
  4397	        return true
  4398	    }
  4399	
  4400	    private fun updatePortraitAuthScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4401	        if (
  4402	            !usesStickyAuthChrome() ||
  4403	            activeMockFlow !in listOf(
  4404	                MockAccountFlow.SignIn,
  4405	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  4406	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  4407	            ) ||
  4408	            activeMockDropdownScroll != null
  4409	        ) {
  4410	            return false
  4411	        }
  4412	
  4413	        val deltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  4414	        val deltaY = event.y - portraitAuthScrollStartY
  4415	        if (!portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
  4416	            if (abs(deltaY) <= touchSlop || abs(deltaY) <= abs(deltaX)) return false
  4417	            if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown && deltaY < 0f) {
  4418	                return false
  4419	            }
  4420	            mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  4421	            portraitAuthScrollMoved = true
  4422	            pressedMockField = null
  4423	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  4424	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4425	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4426	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  4427	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  4428	            expandedMockDropdown = null
  4429	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4430	        }
  4431	
  4432	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  4433	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4434	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4435	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4436	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4437	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4438	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4439	            else -> authSignInMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4440	        }
  4441	        portraitAuthScrollY = (portraitAuthScrollStartOffset - deltaY).coerceIn(0f, maxScrollY)
  4442	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4443	        return true
  4444	    }

  4446	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  4447	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false
  4448	
  4449	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  4450	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  4451	        return mockBackChevronContains(
  4452	            x,
  4453	            y,
  4454	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
  4455	            mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
  4456	            stage
  4457	        )

  4460	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4461	        if (
  4462	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  4463	            mockDragReturnInProgress ||
  4464	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  4465	            !mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown
  4466	        ) {
  4467	            return false
  4468	        }
  4469	
  4470	        val downwardScrollY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  4471	        if (downwardScrollY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  4472	
  4473	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  4474	        return downwardScrollY > abs(dragX)
  4475	    }

  4477	    private fun isMockAuthScreenAtBottom(): Boolean {
  4478	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  4479	
  4480	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  4481	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4482	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4483	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(
  4484	            FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
  4485	        )
  4486	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4487	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4488	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  4489	                stage,
  4490	                yOffset,
  4491	                focusShiftY
  4492	            )
  4493	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> authSignInMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4494	            null -> return false
  4495	        }
  4496	        return portraitAuthScrollY >= maxScrollY - maxOf(1f, stage.scale)
  4497	    }

  4499	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  4500	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  4501	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  4502	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  4503	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  4504	    }

  4506	    private fun mockSignInFieldContains(
  4507	        pointerX: Float,
  4508	        pointerY: Float,
  4509	        fieldX: Float,
  4510	        fieldY: Float,
  4511	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4512	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH,

  4515	    ): Boolean {

  4519	        val visualY = fieldY - (visualHeight - fieldHeight) / 2f
  4520	        val left = stage.left + visualX * stage.scale
  4521	        val top = stage.top + visualY * stage.scale
  4522	        val right = stage.left + (visualX + visualWidth) * stage.scale
  4523	        val bottom = stage.top + (visualY + visualHeight) * stage.scale
  4524	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  4525	    }

  4640	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  4641	        columnX: Float,
  4642	        firstFieldY: Float,
  4643	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  4644	    ): Bounds {
  4645	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  4646	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  4647	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  4648	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  4649	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  4650	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP +
  4651	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) *
  4652	                mockCreateFirstImpressionScale() * 2f
  4653	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  4654	        return Bounds(
  4655	            centerX - side / 2f,
  4656	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  4657	            side,
  4658	            side
  4659	        )
  4660	    }

  4771	    private fun mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(firstFieldY: Float): Float {
  4772	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4773	            val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(mockCreateAvatarColumnX(), firstFieldY)
  4774	            val baseBaseline = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h +
  4775	                MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_USERNAME_DISPLAY_GAP +
  4776	                MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_HERO_CENTER_SHIFT_UP
  4777	            createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE
  4778	            val originalCenterFromBaseline = createUsernameFillPaint.fontMetrics.let {
  4779	                (it.ascent + it.descent) / 2f
  4780	            }
  4781	            createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = mockCreateUsernameTextSize()
  4782	            val scaledCenterFromBaseline = createUsernameFillPaint.fontMetrics.let {
  4783	                (it.ascent + it.descent) / 2f
  4784	            }
  4785	            return baseBaseline + originalCenterFromBaseline - scaledCenterFromBaseline
  4786	        }
  4787	
  4788	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE
  4789	        val usernameTextTopFromBaseline = createUsernameFillPaint.fontMetrics.ascent
  4790	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarTopY(firstFieldY) +
  4791	            MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE
  4792	        return avatarBottomY + MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_SECTION_GAP - usernameTextTopFromBaseline
  4793	    }

  4891	    private fun mockSignInContentStageMetrics(

  4894	        focusShiftY: Float
  4895	    ): StageMetrics {
  4896	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn) return stage
  4897	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4898	            return stage.copy(top = stage.top - portraitAuthScrollY)
  4899	        }
  4900	
  4901	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4902	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4903	        val firstFieldY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4904	        val firstFieldTop = stage.top + firstFieldY * stage.scale
  4905	        return StageMetrics(
  4906	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  4907	            top = firstFieldTop - firstFieldY * contentScale,
  4908	            scale = contentScale
  4909	        )
  4910	    }

  4933	    private fun portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4934	        val title = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4935	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT
  4936	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT
  4937	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT
  4938	            null -> null
  4939	        }
  4940	        if (title != null) {
  4941	            val titleTextSize = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4942	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stage.scale
  4943	            } else {
  4944	                MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stage.scale
  4945	            }
  4946	            val titleBaseline = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  4947	                title,
  4948	                mockPrimaryFormY(),
  4949	                stage.top,
  4950	                stage.scale,
  4951	                titleTextSize
  4952	            )
  4953	            formTitlePaint.textSize = titleTextSize
  4954	            val contentScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) portraitCreateContentScale() else 1f
  4955	            val titleToViewportGap = if (
  4956	                activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && !isPortraitIntroLayout()
  4957	            ) {
  4958	                MOCK_FORM_TITLE_OPTICAL_BOTTOM_OFFSET
  4959	            } else {
  4960	                mockFormFieldGap()
  4961	            }
  4962	            return titleBaseline + formTitlePaint.fontMetrics.descent +
  4963	                titleToViewportGap * stage.scale * contentScale
  4964	        }
  4965	        return stage.top +
  4966	            (portraitLogoVisibleBottom() + MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_GAP) * stage.scale
  4967	    }

  4977	    private fun portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4978	        val stickyBottomHeight = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4979	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE *
  4980	                MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE * stage.scale
  4981	        } else {
  4982	            MOCK_LANDSCAPE_STICKY_BOTTOM_RESERVE * stage.scale
  4983	        }
  4984	        return height - systemBarInsetBottomPx -
  4985	            stickyBottomHeight * portraitStickyBottomMotion()
  4986	    }

  5013	    private fun portraitCreateMaxScrollY(
  5014	        stage: StageMetrics,
  5015	        yOffset: Float,
  5016	        focusShiftY: Float
  5017	    ): Float {
  5018	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5019	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5020	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  5021	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  5022	            avatarSectionY
  5023	        ).let { it.y + it.h }
  5024	        val finalControlBottomY = maxOf(
  5025	            mockDropdownY(MockDropdown.Year, yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  5026	                mockDropdownControlHeight(MockDropdown.Year),
  5027	            mockDropdownY(MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier, yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  5028	                mockDropdownControlHeight(MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier)
  5029	        )
  5030	        val continueButtonBottomY = mockCreateContinueButtonY(yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  5031	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT
  5032	        val contentBottomY = maxOf(avatarBottomY, finalControlBottomY, continueButtonBottomY)
  5033	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + contentBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  5034	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  5035	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  5036	    }

  5087	    private fun mockCreateLandscapeFormTopY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {

  5099	    private fun mockCreateLandscapeHeadingBaselineY(
  5100	        yOffset: Float,
  5101	        focusShiftY: Float
  5102	    ): Float {
  5103	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5104	        val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  5105	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  5106	            avatarSectionY
  5107	        )
  5108	        val avatarCenterY = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f
  5109	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_HEADING_TEXT_SIZE *
  5110	            MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_FIRST_IMPRESSION_SCALE
  5111	        val textPath = Path()
  5112	        val textBounds = RectF()
  5113	        var blockTop = Float.MAX_VALUE
  5114	        var blockBottom = -Float.MAX_VALUE
  5115	        MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_HEADING_TEXT.split('\n').forEachIndexed { index, line ->
  5116	            textPath.reset()
  5117	            formTitlePaint.getTextPath(line, 0, line.length, 0f, 0f, textPath)
  5118	            textPath.computeBounds(textBounds, true)
  5119	            val lineOffsetY = index * MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_HEADING_LINE_HEIGHT *
  5120	                MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_FIRST_IMPRESSION_SCALE
  5121	            blockTop = minOf(blockTop, textBounds.top + lineOffsetY)
  5122	            blockBottom = maxOf(blockBottom, textBounds.bottom + lineOffsetY)
  5123	        }
  5124	        val headlineCenterFromFirstBaseline = (blockTop + blockBottom) / 2f
  5125	        return avatarCenterY - headlineCenterFromFirstBaseline
  5126	    }

  5214	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyBottomY(formTopY: Float): Float {
  5215	        val paragraphs = mockForgotPasswordBodyText().split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  5216	        val copyWidth = mockForgotPasswordCopyWidth()
  5217	        var bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  5218	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  5219	            formTopY + mockForgotPasswordBodyTopGap(),
  5220	            copyWidth,
  5221	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  5222	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  5223	            forceLineBreaks = true
  5224	        )
  5225	        paragraphs.getOrNull(1)?.let { middleText ->
  5226	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  5227	                middleText,
  5228	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  5229	                copyWidth,
  5230	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  5231	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  5232	            )
  5233	        }
  5234	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  5235	        if (bottomText.isNotEmpty()) {
  5236	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  5237	                bottomText,
  5238	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  5239	                copyWidth,
  5240	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  5241	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  5242	            )
  5243	        }
  5244	        return bottomY
  5245	    }

  5255	    private fun mockForgotPasswordHelpBottomY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {

  5259	            mockForgotPasswordHelpText(),
  5260	            helpTopY,
  5261	            mockForgotPasswordFormWidth(),
  5262	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TEXT_SIZE,
  5263	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_LINE_HEIGHT
  5264	        )

  5266	
  5267	    private fun mockParagraphBottomY(
  5268	        text: String,
  5269	        topY: Float,
  5270	        maxWidth: Float,
  5271	        textSize: Float,
  5272	        lineHeight: Float,
  5273	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  5274	    ): Float {
  5275	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize
  5276	        val lineCount = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  5277	            text.split('\n').sumOf { line ->
  5278	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  5279	            }
  5280	        } else {
  5281	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  5282	        }
  5283	        return topY + lineCount * lineHeight
  5284	    }
  5285	
  5286	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  5287	        left: Float,
  5288	        width: Float,
  5289	        labelWidth: Float,
  5290	        boxWidth: Float
  5291	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  5292	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  5293	        val labelX = left + gap
  5294	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  5295	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap
  5296	        return ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  5297	            labelX = labelX,
  5298	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  5299	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  5300	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  5301	        )
  5302	    }
  5303	
  5304	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(
  5305	        usernameTextWidth: Float,
  5306	        passwordTextWidth: Float,
  5307	        baseBoxWidth: Float = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH
  5308	    ): Float {
  5309	        val widestTextWidth = maxOf(usernameTextWidth, passwordTextWidth)
  5310	        val currentHorizontalPadding = ((baseBoxWidth - widestTextWidth) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  5311	        return widestTextWidth + currentHorizontalPadding * 4f
  5312	    }

  5314	    private fun activeMockFields(): List<MockSignInField> {
  5315	        return when (activeMockFlow) {
  5316	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5317	                MOCK_CREATE_ACCOUNT_FIELDS

  5320	            }
  5321	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_FIELDS
  5322	            else -> MOCK_SIGN_IN_FIELDS
  5323	        }
  5324	    }

  5343	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  5344	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  5345	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  5346	
  5347	        val firstColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) mockCreatePortraitColumnX() else mockCreateFormX()
  5348	        val middleColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5349	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()
  5350	        } else {
  5351	            mockCreateLandscapeSecondColumnX()
  5352	        }
  5353	        val enlargedFieldOutwardShift = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5354	            0f
  5355	        } else {
  5356	            MOCK_FORM_WIDTH *
  5357	                (MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_FIRST_IMPRESSION_SCALE - 1f) / 2f
  5358	        }
  5359	        return when (field) {
  5360	            MockSignInField.Username -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5361	                firstColumnX
  5362	            } else {
  5363	                mockCreateLandscapeHeroLeftColumnX()
  5364	            }
  5365	            MockSignInField.Password,
  5366	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> firstColumnX - enlargedFieldOutwardShift
  5367	            MockSignInField.Email,
  5368	            MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> middleColumnX + enlargedFieldOutwardShift
  5369	            MockSignInField.Month -> middleColumnX
  5370	            MockSignInField.Day -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP
  5371	            MockSignInField.Year -> middleColumnX + MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_FIELD_GAP * 2f
  5372	        }
  5373	    }

  5375	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  5376	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  5377	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5378	        }
  5379	        if (
  5380	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  5381	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  5382	            field == MockSignInField.Username
  5383	        ) {
  5384	            return mockCreateLandscapeUsernameFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5385	        }
  5386	
  5387	        val usesSecondSection = activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  5388	            isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  5389	            field in listOf(
  5390	                MockSignInField.Email,
  5391	                MockSignInField.Month,
  5392	                MockSignInField.Day,
  5393	                MockSignInField.Year
  5394	            )
  5395	        val sectionTop = if (usesSecondSection) {
  5396	            mockCreateSecondSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5397	        } else if (
  5398	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && !isPortraitIntroLayout()
  5399	        ) {
  5400	            mockCreateLandscapeFormTopY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5401	        } else {
  5402	            mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  5403	        }
  5404	        val fieldOffsetY = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  5405	            when (field) {
  5406	                MockSignInField.Username -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5407	                    mockCreatePortraitUsernameOffsetY()
  5408	                } else {
  5409	                    0f
  5410	                }
  5411	                MockSignInField.Email -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5412	                    0f
  5413	                } else {
  5414	                    MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_PRIMARY_ROW_SHIFT_DOWN
  5415	                }
  5416	                MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5417	                    0f
  5418	                } else {
  5419	                    mockCreateLandscapeRowOffset(1)
  5420	                }
  5421	                MockSignInField.Password -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5422	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY()
  5423	                } else {
  5424	                    MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_PRIMARY_ROW_SHIFT_DOWN
  5425	                }
  5426	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5427	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY() +
  5428	                        MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  5429	                } else {
  5430	                    mockCreateLandscapeRowOffset(1)
  5431	                }
  5432	                MockSignInField.Month,
  5433	                MockSignInField.Day,
  5434	                MockSignInField.Year -> mockCreateDateRowOffsetY()
  5435	            }
  5436	        } else {
  5437	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0) *
  5438	                (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap())
  5439	        }
  5440	        return sectionTop + fieldOffsetY
  5441	    }
  5442

  5444	        return row.coerceAtLeast(0) *
  5445	            (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_ROW_GAP)
  5446	    }

  5467	    private fun mockBackChevronRowTopY(): Float {
  5468	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
  5469	            val stickyBottomHeight = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5470	                MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE * MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE
  5471	            } else {
  5472	                MOCK_LANDSCAPE_STICKY_BOTTOM_RESERVE
  5473	            }
  5474	            val chevronHeight = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5475	                MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  5476	            } else {
  5477	                1f
  5478	            }
  5479	            val safeBottom = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5480	                portraitSafeBottom()
  5481	            } else {
  5482	                val stage = currentStageMetrics()
  5483	                if (stage == null) {
  5484	                    FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT
  5485	                } else {
  5486	                    (height - systemBarInsetBottomPx - stage.top) / stage.scale
  5487	                }
  5488	            }
  5489	            val settledTop = safeBottom - stickyBottomHeight +
  5490	                (stickyBottomHeight - chevronHeight) / 2f
  5491	            return settledTop + stickyBottomHeight * (1f - portraitStickyBottomMotion())
  5492	        }
  5493	        return ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY() +
  5494	            MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_ROW_TOP_GAP
  5495	    }
  5496

  5498	        if (!usesStickyAuthChrome()) return 0f
  5499	        return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5500	    }

  5708	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  5709	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  5710	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  5711	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  5712	        } else {

  5714	        }

  5716	    }

  5718	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  5719	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  5720	    }

  5722	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  5723	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  5724	    }

  5726	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  5727	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5728	    }

  5730	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  5731	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  5732	    }

  5734	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  5735	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  5736	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  5737	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  5738	    }

  5740	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  5741	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  5742	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  5743	            return
  5744	        }
  5745	
  5746	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  5747	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  5748	    }

  5817	    private fun setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(target: MockForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  5818	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return
  5819	        if (forgotRecoveryTarget == target) {
  5820	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  5821	            return
  5822	        }
  5823	
  5824	        forgotRecoveryTarget = target
  5825	        resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()

  5877	    private fun mockCreateAccountValidationMessage(): String? {
  5878	        val normalizedEmail = emailText.trim()
  5879	        return when {
  5880	            usernameText.trim().length < 3 -> "Username needs at least 3 characters"
  5881	            passwordText.length < 8 -> "Password needs at least 8 characters"
  5882	            passwordText != confirmPasswordText -> "Passwords do not match"
  5883	            !normalizedEmail.contains('@') ||
  5884	                !normalizedEmail.substringAfterLast('@', "").contains('.') -> {
  5885	                "Enter a valid email address"
  5886	            }
  5887	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() && confirmEmailText.trim() != normalizedEmail -> {
  5888	                "Email addresses do not match"
  5889	            }
  5890	            subscriptionTierText == MOCK_FORM_SUBSCRIPTION_TIER_TEXT -> {
  5891	                "Select a subscription tier"
  5892	            }
  5893	            monthText == MOCK_FORM_MONTH_TEXT ||
  5894	                dayText == MOCK_FORM_DAY_TEXT ||
  5895	                yearText == MOCK_FORM_YEAR_TEXT -> {
  5896	                "Select your complete birthday"
  5897	            }
  5898	            else -> null
  5899	        }
  5900	    }

  5917	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  5918	        ) {
  5919	            1f

  5921	            0f
  5922	        }

  5924	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5925	    }

  6005	    }

  6050	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(): Float {
  6051	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return 0f
  6052	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  6053	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  6054	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  6055	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  6056	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE *
  6057	            mockCreateFirstImpressionScale()
  6058	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  6059	    }

  6062	        val slideDistancePx = (slideDistance * stageScale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  6063	        return (abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) / slideDistancePx).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6064	    }

  6108	                mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] ?: 0f,

  6207	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  6208	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  6209	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  6210	        return if (
  6211	            focusedMockField != null ||
  6212	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  6213	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  6214	        ) {
  6215	            1f
  6216	        } else {
  6217	            0f
  6218	        }
  6219	    }
  6220	
  6221	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  6222	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  6223	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  6224	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  6225	    }
  6226	
  6227	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  6228	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  6229	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  6230	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  6231	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  6232	        }
  6233	
  6234	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  6235	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  6236	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  6237	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  6238	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  6239	    }

  6243	        if (expandedMockDropdown != null) return 1f

  6245	        return if (focusedMockField == awaitingField) 1f else 0f
  6246	    }

  6255	            focusedMockField != null ||
  6256	            activeMockFields().any { mockSignInFieldText(it).isNotEmpty() }

  6309	    private fun mockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField): String {
  6310	        return when (field) {

  6312	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText

  6314	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText
  6315	            MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> confirmEmailText
  6316	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText
