  6322	    private fun mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field: MockSignInField): String {
  6323	        return when (field) {
  6324	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText
  6325	            MockSignInField.Password -> "*".repeat(passwordText.length)
  6326	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> "*".repeat(confirmPasswordText.length)
  6327	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText
  6328	            MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> confirmEmailText
  6329	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText
  6330	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText
  6331	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText
  6332	        }
  6333	    }
  6334	
  6335	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {
  6336	        val limitedText = text.take(MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS)
  6337	        val usernameWasEmpty = usernameText.isEmpty()
  6338	        when (field) {
  6339	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
  6340	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  6341	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  6342	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  6343	            MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> confirmEmailText = limitedText
  6344	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  6345	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  6346	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  6347	        }
  6348	        if (field == MockSignInField.Username && usernameWasEmpty != usernameText.isEmpty()) {
  6349	            restartMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
  6350	        }
  6351	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  6352	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6353	        }
  6354	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6355	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  6356	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  6357	        }
  6358	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  6359	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  6360	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  6361	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6362	    }
  6363	
  6364	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  6365	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  6366	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  6367	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  6368	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  6369	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  6370	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  6371	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  6372	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  6373	        activeComposingText = ""
  6374	        return true
  6375	    }
  6376	
  6377	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  6378	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  6379	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  6380	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  6381	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  6382	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  6383	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  6384	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  6385	        activeComposingText = composingText
  6386	        return true
  6387	    }
  6388	
  6389	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  6390	        activeComposingText = ""
  6391	        return true
  6392	    }
  6393	
  6394	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  6395	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  6396	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  6397	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  6398	            activeComposingText = ""
  6399	            return true
  6400	        }
  6401	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  6402	        activeComposingText = ""
  6403	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  6404	        return true
  6405	    }
  6406	
  6407	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  6408	        activeComposingText = ""
  6409	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  6410	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  6411	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  6412	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  6413	        } else if (
  6414	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  6415	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  6416	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  6417	        ) {
  6418	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6419	        } else {
  6420	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  6421	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  6422	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  6423	        }
  6424	        return true
  6425	    }
  6426	
  6427	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  6428	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  6429	        return when (keyCode) {
  6430	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  6431	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  6432	            else -> {
  6433	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  6434	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  6435	            }
  6436	        }
  6437	    }
  6438	
  6439	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  6440	        return buildString {
  6441	            input.forEach { character ->
  6442	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  6443	                    append(character)
  6444	                }
  6445	            }
  6446	        }
  6447	    }
  6448	
  6449	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  6450	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  6451	
  6452	        var startIndex = 0
  6453	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  6454	            startIndex += 1
  6455	        }
  6456	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  6457	    }
  6458	
  6459	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  6460	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  6461	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  6462	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  6463	                lines += ""
  6464	            }
  6465	
  6466	            var currentLine = ""
  6467	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  6468	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  6469	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  6470	                .forEach { word ->
  6471	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  6472	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  6473	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  6474	                    } else {
  6475	                        lines += currentLine
  6476	                        currentLine = word
  6477	                    }
  6478	                }
  6479	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  6480	                lines += currentLine
  6481	            }
  6482	        }
  6483	        return lines
  6484	    }
  6485	
  6486	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  6487	        return when {
  6488	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  6489	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  6490	            else -> current
  6491	        }
  6492	    }
  6493	
  6494	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  6495	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  6496	
  6497	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  6498	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  6499	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  6500	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  6501	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  6502	        return StageMetrics(
  6503	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  6504	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  6505	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  6506	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  6507	            scale = stageScale
  6508	        )
  6509	    }

  6513	    private fun portraitIntroAmount(): Float {
  6514	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return 0f
  6515	        // Keep the portrait welcome composition fixed while an auth panel slides in.
  6516	        return 1f
  6517	    }

  6519	    private fun portraitDesignScale(): Float {
  6520	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return 1f
  6521	
  6522	        val widthScale = width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
  6523	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return widthScale
  6524	
  6525	        val referenceDesignHeight = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * PORTRAIT_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_TO_WIDTH
  6526	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  6527	    }

  6659	        val rawBottomRoom = portraitSafeBottom() -
  6660	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)
  6661	        return (rawTopRoom + rawBottomRoom) / 2f
  6662	    }

  6667	            (PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_DESIGN_HEIGHT - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT))
  6668	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6669	    }

  6744	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  6745	        if (
  6746	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  6747	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  6748	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  6749	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  6750	        ) {
  6751	            return
  6752	        }
  6753	
  6754	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  6755	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  6756	        activeComposingText = ""
  6757	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  6758	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  6759	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  6760	        portraitAuthScrollY = 0f
  6761	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  6762	        pressedMockField = null
  6763	        pressedRememberMe = false
  6764	        pressedBackChevron = false
  6765	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  6766	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  6767	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  6768	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  6769	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  6770	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  6771	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  6772	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  6773	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  6774	        pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  6775	        showCreateAccountValidationWarning = false
  6776	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  6777	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  6778	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  6779	            emailText = ""
  6780	            confirmEmailText = ""
  6781	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6782	        }
  6783	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  6784	        activeMockFlow = flow
  6785	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  6786	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  6787	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  6788	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  6789	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  6790	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6791	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6792	    }

  6799	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  6800	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  6801	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6802	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6803	    }

  6888	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  6889	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  6890	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  6891	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6892	        return lerpFloat(
  6893	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  6894	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  6895	            progress
  6896	        )
  6897	    }
  6898	
  6899	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  6900	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  6901	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  6902	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6903	    }
  6904	
  6905	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  6906	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  6907	    }
  6908	
  6909	    private fun finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded() {
  6910	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return
  6911	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  6912	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  6913	    }
  6914	
  6915	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  6916	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  6917	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  6918	    }
  6919	
  6920	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  6921	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  6922	    }
  6923	
  6924	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  6925	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  6926	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  6927	            .roundToInt()
  6928	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  6929	            .toLong()
  6930	    }
  6931	
  6932	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  6933	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  6934	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  6935	
  6936	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  6937	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  6938	            activeMockFlow = null
  6939	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  6940	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  6941	            pressedMockField = null
  6942	            pressedRememberMe = false
  6943	            pressedBackChevron = false
  6944	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  6945	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  6946	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  6947	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  6948	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  6949	            pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  6950	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  6951	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  6952	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  6953	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6954	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  6955	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6956	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6957	            return
  6958	        }
  6959	
  6960	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  6961	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  6962	    }

  6964	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  6965	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  6966	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  6967	        }
  6968	
  6969	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  6970	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  6971	        }
  6972	
  6973	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  6974	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  6975	        }
  6976	
  6977	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  6978	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  6979	        }
  6980	
  6981	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  6982	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  6983	        }
  6984	
  6985	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  6986	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  6987	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  6988	        }
  6989	
  6990	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  6991	            return ""
  6992	        }
  6993	
  6994	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  6995	            return ""
  6996	        }
  6997	
  6998	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  6999	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  7000	        }
  7001	
  7002	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  7003	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  7004	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  7005	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  7006	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  7007	                else -> {
  7008	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  7009	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  7010	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  7011	                    } else {
  7012	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  7013	                    }
  7014	                }
  7015	            }
  7016	        }
  7017	    }
  7018	}

  7190	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  7191	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  7192	}
  7193	
  7194	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  7195	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  7196	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7197	}
  7198	
  7199	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  7200	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  7201	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7202	}
  7203	
  7204	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  7205	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  7206	}
  7207	
  7208	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  7209	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  7210	}
  7211	
  7212	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  7213	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7214	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  7215	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  7216	    return base + bounce
  7217	}
  7218	
  7219	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  7220	
  7221	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  7222	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  7223	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  7224	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  7225	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  7226	)
  7227	
  7228	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  7229	    x = bounds.x,
  7230	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  7231	    w = bounds.w,
  7232	    h = bounds.h
  7233	)
  7234	
  7235	private fun scaleBoundsFromTopCenter(bounds: Bounds, scale: Float): Bounds {
  7236	    val scaledWidth = bounds.w * scale
  7237	    return Bounds(
  7238	        x = bounds.x + (bounds.w - scaledWidth) / 2f,
  7239	        y = bounds.y,
  7240	        w = scaledWidth,
  7241	        h = bounds.h * scale
  7242	    )
  7243	}
  7244	
  7245	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  7246	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7247	    return Color.argb(
  7248	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  7249	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  7250	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  7251	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  7252	    )
  7253	}
  7254	
  7255	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  7256	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  7257	}
  7258	
  7259	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  7260	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  7261	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  7262	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  7263	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  7264	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  7265	    return output
  7266	}
  7267	
  7268	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  7269	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  7270	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  7271	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  7272	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  7273	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  7274	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  7275	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  7276	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  7277	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  7278	    return output
  7279	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	### Standard tablet emulator
    32	
    33	Use the guarded launcher for the persistent low-load QA profile:
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	./start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
    37	```
    38	
    39	The launcher requests and verifies `1280x800` at `160 dpi`, two guest CPU cores,
    40	host GPU acceleration, and a frameless display with direct host-pointer mapping
    41	before opening Cinerific. It refuses to continue if the running AVD does not match.
    42	Emulator logs are written to
    43	`~/Library/Logs/Cinerific/standard-emulator.log`.
    44	
    45	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    46	
    47	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    48	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    49	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	set -Eeuo pipefail
     3	
     4	ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     5	SDK="${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-$HOME/Library/Android/sdk}}"
     6	EMU="${SDK}/emulator/emulator"
     7	ADB="${SDK}/platform-tools/adb"
     8	AVD="Cinerific_Standard_Tablet"
     9	AVD_CONFIG="$HOME/.android/avd/${AVD}.avd/config.ini"
    10	DEVICE="emulator-5554"
    11	PORT="5554"
    12	APP_PACKAGE="com.prahlin.cinerific"
    13	APP_ACTIVITY="${APP_PACKAGE}/.MainActivity"
    14	EXPECTED_SIZE="1280x800"
    15	EXPECTED_DENSITY="160"
    16	EXPECTED_CORES="2"
    17	EXPECTED_FRAME="no"
    18	BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS="${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:-180}"
    19	LOG_DIR="$HOME/Library/Logs/Cinerific"
    20	LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.log"
    21	ERROR_LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.err.log"
    22	LAUNCHD_LABEL="com.cinerific.standardavd"
    23	LAUNCHD_PLIST="$ROOT/launchd/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}.plist"
    24	
    25	fail() {
    26	  printf 'ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    27	  exit 1
    28	}
    29	
    30	[[ -x "$EMU" ]] || fail "Android emulator not found at $EMU"
    31	[[ -x "$ADB" ]] || fail "adb not found at $ADB"
    32	[[ -f "$LAUNCHD_PLIST" ]] || fail "LaunchAgent not found at $LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    33	[[ -f "$AVD_CONFIG" ]] || fail "AVD configuration not found at $AVD_CONFIG"
    34	"$EMU" -list-avds | grep -qx "$AVD" || fail "AVD $AVD was not found"
    35	
    36	config_frame="$(sed -n 's/^showDeviceFrame=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    37	[[ "$config_frame" == "$EXPECTED_FRAME" ]] || fail "Expected a frameless AVD, got showDeviceFrame=$config_frame"
    38	
    39	mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
    40	
    41	device_is_connected() {
    42	  "$ADB" devices | awk -v device="$DEVICE" \
    43	    '$1 == device && $2 == "device" { found = 1 } END { exit !found }'
    44	}
    45	
    46	if device_is_connected; then
    47	  actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    48	  [[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "$DEVICE is running $actual_avd, not $AVD"
    49	  printf '%s is already running.\n' "$AVD"
    50	else
    51	  printf 'Starting %s with the low-load QA profile...\n' "$AVD"
    52	  launchctl bootout "gui/$(id -u)/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    53	  : > "$LOG"
    54	  : > "$ERROR_LOG"
    55	  launchctl bootstrap "gui/$(id -u)" "$LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    56	fi
    57	
    58	deadline=$((SECONDS + BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS))
    59	until device_is_connected; do
    60	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Emulator did not connect within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    61	  sleep 2
    62	done
    63	
    64	until [[ "$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell getprop sys.boot_completed 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')" == "1" ]]; do
    65	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Android did not finish booting within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    66	  sleep 2
    67	done
    68	
    69	actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    70	actual_size="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm size | sed -n 's/^Physical size: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    71	actual_density="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm density | sed -n 's/^Physical density: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    72	actual_cores="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell 'grep -c "^processor" /proc/cpuinfo' | tr -d '\r')"
    73	
    74	[[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "Expected $AVD, got $actual_avd"
    75	[[ "$actual_size" == "$EXPECTED_SIZE" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_SIZE, got $actual_size"
    76	[[ "$actual_density" == "$EXPECTED_DENSITY" ]] || fail "Expected density $EXPECTED_DENSITY, got $actual_density"
    77	[[ "$actual_cores" == "$EXPECTED_CORES" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_CORES cores, got $actual_cores"
    78	
    79	sleep 10
    80	device_is_connected || fail "Emulator disconnected after boot; see $LOG"
    81	
    82	if "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell pm path "$APP_PACKAGE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    83	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am force-stop "$APP_PACKAGE"
    84	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am start -n "$APP_ACTIVITY" >/dev/null
    85	  printf 'Cinerific launched on %s.\n' "$DEVICE"
    86	else
    87	  printf 'Cinerific is not installed on %s; emulator verification still passed.\n' "$DEVICE"
    88	fi
    89	
    90	printf 'Verified: %s @ %s dpi, %s cores, host GPU.\n' \
    91	  "$actual_size" "$actual_density" "$actual_cores"
    92	printf 'Emulator log: %s\n' "$LOG"


============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/launchd/com.cinerific.standardavd.plist" \
   177	    "$PROJECT_DIR/start_cinerific_android_standard.sh" \
   178	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   179	  do
   180	    print_file "$file"
   181	  done
   182	
   183	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   184	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — EMULATOR SKINS" "$PROJECT_DIR/emulator/skins"
   185	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   186	
   187	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   188	
   189	
   190	============================================================
   191	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   192	============================================================
   193	
   194	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   195	- .git
   196	- .gradle
   197	- .idea
   198	- app/build
   199	- build
   200	- .kotlin
   201	- .tmp
   202	- .cache
   203	- .DS_Store
   204	- local.properties
   205	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   206	- *.jks
   207	- *.keystore
   208	
   209	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   210	
   211	EOF
   212	}
   213	
   214	write_snapshot
   215	
   216	echo "Done."
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   219	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   220	echo ""
   221	echo "Open it with:"
   222	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
